`README.md`:

```md
# android-renef-mcp

An MCP server for [Renef](https://renef.io), a Frida-like dynamic instrumentation toolkit for Android ARM64. With it, an AI agent can attach to a process on a rooted device (or spawn one, held paused at startup), run Lua inside the target, hook native and Java methods, read and write process memory, and collect hook output.

Renef runs Lua instead of JavaScript, the API is its own (`hook()`, `Module.*`, `Memory.*`, `Java.use`, `Syscall.trace`), and nothing frida-related has to be on the device. Just the `renef_server` and a rooted phone.

```
┌──────────────┐   stdio/MCP   ┌─────────────┐  TCP :1907   ┌───────────────┐
│ MCP client   │ ◄───────────► │  renef-mcp  │ ◄──────────► │ renef_server  │
│ (AI agent)   │               │ (this pkg)  │  (adb fwd)   │ on device(adb)│
└──────────────┘               └─────────────┘              └──────┬────────┘
                                                                   │ agent socket
                                                                   ▼
                                                          ┌─────────────────┐
                                                          │ libagent.so     │
                                                          │ inside target   │
                                                          │ app (Lua VM)    │
                                                          └─────────────────┘
```

## Differences from renef-mcp

There is already an MCP server for Renef:
[ahmeth4n/renef-mcp](https://github.com/ahmeth4n/renef-mcp) on PyPI. This package exists because its core is built differently, and after running both against a real device, differently in
ways that matter:

| | android-renef-mcp (this package) | renef-mcp (ahmeth4n, 0.3.0) |
|---|---|---|
| Talking to the device | Speaks the `renef_server` TCP protocol directly, over an adb forward | Launches the desktop `renef` CLI as a subprocess and scrapes its `renef>` prompt |
| Multi-line Lua | Native, sent in one write | Broken; needs a second hex-encoding tool as a workaround |
| Response framing | Every exec carries a nonce marker, runs exactly once, and times out loudly | Blind waits for the prompt; a hook firing mid-response bleeds into the next tool's output |
| Spawn | Real startup gating (ptrace pause), first exec resumes it | Sends the legacy `--hook=` flag the server strips and ignores |
| Memory search | `'DEADBEEF'` searches those bytes; spaced and wildcard patterns validated | `'DEADBEEF'` searches the ASCII text of that string — a silent wrong answer |
| Native hooks | Lua callbacks (`onEnter`/`onLeave`), PLT/GOT via `caller=` | The `hookn` command, which installs no callbacks |
| Tests | 45-check end-to-end suite over real MCP stdio | None |

Upstream also ships ~9.5 MB of prebuilt host binaries and runs on Linux/macOS
only; this package is ~100 KB of Python and runs anywhere Python and adb do.

Credit where due: the server lifecycle tools, framework detection, the Lua
script library and the SSL-pinning knowledge base here are adapted from
upstream's work (MIT, credited in their sections below).

## Tools (38)

**Status and discovery** (no target needed)

- `renef_status()` — adb devices, port forward, server ping/pong, session state
- `renef_list_apps(third_party_only)` — installed packages
- `renef_list_processes()` — running processes, for `renef_attach`

**Server lifecycle** (no session needed)

- `renef_server_deploy(source)` — push `renef_server` + `libagent.so` to the
  device, set permissions and the SELinux context the agent needs, verify by md5.
  With the default `source="auto"` it prefers a local Renef build and otherwise
  downloads the pinned, CI-built binaries (SHA-verified) — no NDK required
- `renef_server_start()` / `renef_server_stop()` / `renef_server_restart()` —
  root-managed lifecycle with ping verification
- `renef_server_logs(lines)` — tail the server log on the device

**Session**

- `renef_attach(pid)` — inject the agent into a running process
- `renef_spawn(package)` — spawn an app gated at startup (ptrace pause) and
  inject the agent. The first exec auto-resumes it, or call `renef_resume`
  without a script
- `renef_resume()` — resume a spawn-gated process
- `renef_disconnect()` — close the session; the target keeps running

**Execution**

- `renef_exec(lua)` — run Lua in the target and get back its `print()` output.
  The workhorse, and multi-line Lua is fine
- `renef_load_script(path)` — run a Lua file from the host machine

**Memory**

- `renef_mem_scan(pattern, module)` — scan for a hex pattern. Prefer `module="libfoo.so"`, which uses Lua `Memory.search` and takes spaces and `??` wildcards. Without a module it's the upstream full-process scan: compact hex only, and big processes can outrun the server's ~10s response cap
- `renef_mem_read(addr, size)` — hexdump, up to 4096 bytes
- `renef_mem_read_value(addr, size, signed)` — integer read, 1/2/4/8 bytes
- `renef_mem_read_string(addr, max_len)` — NUL-terminated C string
- `renef_mem_write(addr, hex_bytes)` — write raw bytes
- `renef_mem_write_value(addr, value, size)` — write an integer, verified by
  reading it back
- `renef_mem_patch(addr, hex_bytes)` — like `mem_write` but via `Memory.patch`,
  which handles `mprotect` itself, so read-only code pages are fair game

**Modules**

- `renef_module_list()` and `renef_module_find(name)` — loaded libraries, base
  addresses
- `renef_module_exports(name, filter, limit)` / `renef_module_symbols(name, filter, limit)`
- `renef_sections(lib)` — ELF sections of a loaded library
- `renef_framework_detect()` — flags Flutter, React Native, Unity, Xamarin or
  Cordova by their characteristic libraries
- `renef_hook_generate(lib, symbol_or_offset)` — server-generated Lua hook
  template; give it a symbol and it resolves the offset first

**Hooks**

- `renef_hook_native(lib, offset, on_enter, on_leave, caller)` — trampoline hook
  by default; pass `caller="libtarget.so"` for the PLT/GOT backend. Callbacks
  are Lua statements over `args[0..7]` (onEnter) and `retval` (onLeave). Find
  offsets with `renef_module_exports` first
- `renef_hook_java(class, method, signature, on_enter, on_leave)` — ART method
  hook. Classes in slash form (`com/example/Class`), JNI signatures. `args[0]`
  is the ArtMethod pointer, `args[1]` is `this`, `args[2..]` are the parameters,
  and `args.skip = true` bypasses a void method
- `renef_list_hooks()` / `renef_unhook(hook_id | "all")` — native hooks only.
  Java hooks aren't in the upstream registry; they're cleared on the next
  attach/spawn
- `renef_watch(seconds)` — capture hook/strace output for a window. Blocks the
  session exclusively, capped at 60s, and shows nothing if the app does nothing

**Diagnostics**

- `renef_backtrace()` — current-thread call stack

**Script library** (vendored from [ahmeth4n/renef-mcp](https://github.com/ahmeth4n/renef-mcp), MIT)

- `renef_list_scripts()` — 18 bundled examples: hook demos, tracing, and working
  bypasses like `flutter_ssl`, `ssl_unpin` and `root_bypass`
- `renef_get_script(name)` — the Lua source, ready for `renef_load_script`

**Technique knowledge base** (vendored from ahmeth4n/renef-mcp, MIT)

- `renef_technique_list()` — categories and platforms; currently `ssl_pinning`
  for android_native, flutter, react_native, unity, xamarin and cordova_ionic
- `renef_technique(category, platform)` — the assembled guide, e.g.
  `renef_technique("ssl_pinning", "flutter")`

Resources: `renef://status` for live chain status, `renef://docs/lua-api` for the
full Lua API reference (read from the Renef checkout). There's also a
`renef_expert(task)` prompt that keeps agents from accidentally writing Frida
code.

## Usage

Requirements: Python 3.10+, `adb`, and a rooted ARM64 device. The Renef binaries
themselves you have two ways to get:

- **No build needed.** `renef_server_deploy()` downloads `renef_server` and `libagent.so` that CI builds from the pinned renef source (see
  `renef_mcp/BINARIES_PIN`), SHA-verified against the release's checksums, and caches them under `~/.cache/android-renef-mcp/`.
- **Build your own.** If you want binaries from your own checkout (useful while
  hacking on Renef itself):

```bash
git clone https://github.com/FlyPhantomFly/android-renef-mcp
cd android-renef-mcp
make                # produces build/android/renef_server and libagent.so
```

Then install this package and register it with your MCP client. Once it's on
PyPI this is just:

```bash
pip install android-renef-mcp
```
```bash
claude mcp add renef -- android-renef-mcp
```

From a source checkout meanwhile:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/FlyPhantomFly/android-renef-mcp
cd android-renef-mcp
uv sync
claude mcp add renef -- uv run --directory ~/github/android-renef-mcp android-renef-mcp
```

Or the JSON form most clients accept:

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "renef": {
      "command": "uv",
      "args": ["run", "--directory", "/home/<you>/android-renef-mcp", "android-renef-mcp"]
    }
  }
}
```

After that you don't need to touch adb yourself: `renef_server_deploy()` gets
the binaries (downloaded or local) onto the device, `renef_server_start()`
starts the server as root and sets up the port forward, and `renef_status()`
confirms the chain. If you'd rather do it
by hand, the old route still works:

```bash
adb shell "su -c '/data/local/tmp/renef_server'"   # start the server (root)
```

The server takes no arguments. Environment variables, should you need them:
`RENEF_HOME` (Renef checkout, used for the Lua API docs resource; default
`~/github/renef`), `RENEF_BIN_DIR` (where `renef_server_deploy` finds the built
binaries; default `<RENEF_HOME>/build/android`), `RENEF_HOST`/`RENEF_PORT`
(server address, default `127.0.0.1:1907` over the adb forward) and `RENEF_ADB`
(adb binary path).

A session then looks like this:

```
renef_status
renef_spawn("com.example.app")
renef_module_exports("libtarget.so", filter="crypt")
renef_hook_native("libtarget.so", "0x1a2b3",
                  on_enter="print('arg:', Memory.readString(args[0]))")
renef_watch(5)
renef_unhook("all")
renef_disconnect()
```

## Planned integration

This server is planned for integration into
[android-re-mcp](https://github.com/FlyPhantomFly/android-re-mcp) as its
Frida-alternative dynamic instrumentation layer. Until then, this repo is the
standalone source.

documents come from [ahmeth4n/renef-mcp](https://github.com/ahmeth4n/renef-mcp)
and are MIT (© 2025 Byteria; license texts live in `renef_mcp/scripts/` and
`renef_mcp/techniques/`).

```toml
[project]
name = "android-renef-mcp"
version = "0.1.2"
description = "MCP server for Renef - dynamic instrumentation toolkit for Android ARM64"
readme = "README.md"
requires-python = ">=3.10"
license = { text = "Apache-2.0" }
authors = [{ name = "FlyPhantomFly" }]
keywords = ["renef", "mcp", "android", "instrumentation", "reverse-engineering", "frida", "lua"]
dependencies = [
    "mcp>=1.12.0,<2",
]

[project.scripts]
android-renef-mcp = "renef_mcp.server:main"

[project.urls]
Homepage = "https://renef.io"
Repository = "https://github.com/FlyPhantomFly/android-renef-mcp"
Issues = "https://github.com/FlyPhantomFly/android-renef-mcp/issues"

[build-system]
requires = ["hatchling"]
build-backend = "hatchling.build"

[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]
packages = ["renef_mcp"]

```

`renef_mcp/__init__.py`:

```py
"""android-renef-mcp: MCP server exposing Renef (Android dynamic instrumentation) to AI agents."""

from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version

try:
    __version__ = version("android-renef-mcp")
except PackageNotFoundError:
    # Keep in sync with pyproject.toml [project].version.
    __version__ = "0.1.2"

```

`renef_mcp/__main__.py`:

```py
import sys

from renef_mcp.server import main

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`renef_mcp/adb.py`:

```py
"""adb + raw-TCP helpers for talking to renef_server."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import socket
import subprocess

from renef_mcp import config


class AdbError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def run_adb(*args: str, timeout: float = 20.0) -> str:
    """Run an adb command, raise AdbError on failure. Returns stdout."""
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(
            [config.adb_binary(), *args],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=timeout,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        raise AdbError(f"adb binary not found: {config.adb_binary()!r}") from e
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
        raise AdbError(f"adb {' '.join(args)} timed out") from e
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise AdbError(f"adb {' '.join(args)} failed: {proc.stderr.strip() or proc.stdout.strip()}")
    return proc.stdout


def device_serials() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Serials of devices in 'device' state (always fresh: a device can be attached later)."""
    out = run_adb("devices")
    serials = []
    for line in out.splitlines()[1:]:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) >= 2 and parts[1] == "device":
            serials.append(parts[0])
    return tuple(serials)


def first_device() -> str | None:
    serials = device_serials()
    return serials[0] if serials else None


def ensure_forward() -> str:
    """Make sure tcp:<RENEF_PORT> is forwarded to the device server socket.

    Returns a short human description of what was done. Skips entirely when
    RENEF_HOST points at a remote (non-loopback) server.
    """
    if not config.uses_local_forward():
        return f"direct TCP mode (RENEF_HOST={config.host()}), no adb forward needed"

    port = config.port()
    local = f"tcp:{port}"
    out = run_adb("forward", "--list")
    for line in out.splitlines():
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) >= 3 and parts[1] == local:
            return f"adb forward already set: {parts[0]} {local} -> {parts[2]}"

    serial = first_device()
    if serial is None:
        raise AdbError(
            "No adb device in 'device' state found. Connect a device and enable USB debugging."
        )
    run_adb("-s", serial, "forward", local, config.FORWARD_REMOTE)
    return f"adb forward set: {serial} {local} -> {config.FORWARD_REMOTE}"


def ping_server(timeout: float = 3.0) -> bool:
    """Raw protocol ping: connect to renef_server, send 'ping', expect 'pong'."""
    try:
        with socket.create_connection((config.host(), config.port()), timeout=timeout) as s:
            s.settimeout(timeout)
            s.sendall(b"ping\n")
            data = s.recv(64)
            return b"pong" in data
    except OSError:
        return False


def raw_command(command: str, timeout: float = 5.0) -> str:
    """Send a raw server-level command (e.g. 'resume') and collect the response."""
    with socket.create_connection((config.host(), config.port()), timeout=timeout) as s:
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        s.sendall(command.rstrip("\n").encode() + b"\n")
        chunks = []
        try:
            while True:
                data = s.recv(4096)
                if not data:
                    break
                chunks.append(data.decode(errors="replace"))
                if b"\n" in data:
                    break
        except socket.timeout:
            pass
        return "".join(chunks).strip()


def list_packages(third_party_only: bool = False) -> list[str]:
    """Installed packages via adb (no server required)."""
    args = ["shell", "pm", "list", "packages"]
    if third_party_only:
        args.append("-3")
    out = run_adb(*args, timeout=30.0)
    pkgs = []
    for line in out.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if line.startswith("package:"):
            pkgs.append(line[len("package:") :])
    return sorted(pkgs)


def list_processes(limit: int = 300) -> list[dict]:
    """Running userland processes via adb: [{'pid': int, 'name': str}, ...]."""
    out = run_adb("shell", "ps", "-A", "-o", "PID,NAME", timeout=30.0)
    procs = []
    for line in out.splitlines()[1:]:
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        m = re.match(r"^\s*(\d+)\s+(\S+)$", line)
        if not m:
            continue
        pid_s, name = m.group(1), m.group(2)
        if name.startswith("["):  # kernel thread
            continue
        procs.append({"pid": int(pid_s), "name": name})
        if len(procs) >= limit:
            break
    return procs

```

`renef_mcp/client.py`:

```py
"""Pure-Python client for renef_server (text protocol over TCP, one connection).

Protocol notes (learned from renef server sources):
- The device server is single-client: one connection handles all commands.
- Commands are text lines; multi-line payloads (Lua scripts) are fine when sent
  as one write ending in a single trailing "\\n".
- `exec <lua>` responses are: Lua print output ... then "✓ Lua executed" (the
  marker the server relay greps for) and finally "OK".  We wrap user Lua with a
  per-exec nonce so the client can wait for *our* completion marker instead of
  guessing from a quiet period.  A quiet-period guess caused scripts with a
  >0.35s silent gap to be returned early, retried and executed twice.
- If the completion marker never arrives, the client returns an explicit
  timeout and does NOT retry the script (retrying is unsafe for non-idempotent
  instrumentation).  The next agent command performs a no-op resync to discard
  any late output.
- `ms` responses end after the JSON result, but the upstream server caps its
  wait at 10s.  A full-process scan can exceed that; the late JSON then poisons
  the next command unless it is drained.  We set a resync flag on timeout.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import socket
import time
from typing import Optional

from renef_mcp import adb, config
from renef_mcp.lua import normalize_hex_pattern, quote

# Exact markers the server relay greps for (byte sequences, UTF-8):
MARK_OK = "✓ Lua executed"       # early-exit success marker
MARK_ERR_PREFIX = "✗ Lua"        # early-exit error marker

_EXEC_WRAPPER_HEAD = "local __renef_ok, __renef_err = pcall(function()\n"
_EXEC_WRAPPER_TAIL_TMPL = (
    "\nend)\n"
    'if not __renef_ok then print("' + MARK_ERR_PREFIX + ' error: " .. tostring(__renef_err)) end\n'
    'print("' + MARK_OK + ' [{nonce}]")\n'
)

FIRST_LINE_TERMINATORS = ("OK", "FAIL", "ERROR")
_PACKAGE_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_]+(?:\.[A-Za-z0-9_]+)+$")


def validate_package(package: str) -> str:
    """Validate an Android package name before it reaches the device shell.

    Upstream builds ``monkey -p <package>`` / ``pidof <package>`` shell strings,
    so a package name with spaces or metacharacters is a command-injection
    vector.  This rejects it on the host, before any server command is sent.
    """
    package = str(package).strip()
    if not _PACKAGE_RE.fullmatch(package):
        raise ValueError(
            f"invalid package name {package!r}; expected a dotted Android package "
            "such as com.example.app"
        )
    return package


class RenefError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class RenefClient:
    """One TCP connection to renef_server. Thread-confine it (manager lock)."""

    def __init__(self, host: Optional[str] = None, port: Optional[int] = None):
        self.host = host or config.host()
        self.port = port or config.port()
        self._sock: Optional[socket.socket] = None
        self.pid: int = -1
        self.target_label: str = ""
        # Set when a command timed out and late agent output may still arrive.
        self._needs_resync: bool = False

    # -- connection ----------------------------------------------------------

    def connected(self) -> bool:
        return self._sock is not None

    def connect(self) -> None:
        if self._sock is not None:
            return
        if config.uses_local_forward():
            # raises AdbError on hard failures (no device etc.)
            adb.ensure_forward()
        try:
            self._sock = socket.create_connection((self.host, self.port), timeout=5)
            self._sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
        except OSError as e:
            raise RenefError(
                f"cannot connect to renef_server at {self.host}:{self.port} ({e}). "
                "Is the server running on the device: adb shell \"su -c '/data/local/tmp/renef_server'\"?"
            ) from e

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self._sock is not None:
            try:
                self._sock.close()
            except OSError:
                pass
            self._sock = None
        self.pid = -1
        self.target_label = ""
        self._needs_resync = False

    # -- low-level read ------------------------------------------------------

    def _read_response(
        self,
        quiet_seconds: float = 0.3,
        total_cap: float = 30.0,
        first_line_fast: bool = False,
        end_marker: Optional[str] = None,
        stall_seconds: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Read until completion marker / quiet-period / cap. Returns text.

        When ``end_marker`` is set, the loop waits for that exact marker (not a
        quiet gap), up to ``total_cap``.  ``stall_seconds`` aborts early when no
        data at all has arrived for that long, so a wedged agent does not burn
        the full deadline.  After the marker arrives we keep draining briefly
        for the agent's trailing "OK" line.
        """
        assert self._sock is not None
        sock = self._sock
        sock.settimeout(0.1)
        chunks: list[bytes] = []
        start = time.time()
        last_activity = start
        marker_seen_at: Optional[float] = None
        quiet_since: Optional[float] = None
        first_line_done = False

        while True:
            now = time.time()
            if now - start > total_cap:
                break

            text_so_far = b"".join(chunks).decode(errors="replace")

            if end_marker is not None:
                if end_marker in text_so_far:
                    if marker_seen_at is None:
                        marker_seen_at = now
                    if now - marker_seen_at >= 0.3:
                        break
                elif now - last_activity >= (stall_seconds if stall_seconds is not None else total_cap):
                    break
            else:
                if quiet_since is not None and now - quiet_since >= quiet_seconds:
                    break
                if marker_seen_at is not None and now - marker_seen_at >= 0.3:
                    break

            try:
                data = sock.recv(65536)
            except socket.timeout:
                if chunks and quiet_since is None:
                    quiet_since = now
                if not end_marker and not first_line_done and first_line_fast and chunks:
                    if self._has_first_line_terminator(text_so_far):
                        break
                continue
            except OSError:
                break

            if not data:
                break

            chunks.append(data)
            last_activity = time.time()
            quiet_since = None
            text_so_far = b"".join(chunks).decode(errors="replace")

            if not first_line_done:
                first_line_done = "\n" in text_so_far
                if first_line_fast and self._has_first_line_terminator(text_so_far):
                    # terminator line fully received?
                    if "\n" in text_so_far:
                        break

            if end_marker is None and marker_seen_at is None and (
                MARK_OK in text_so_far or MARK_ERR_PREFIX in text_so_far
            ):
                # Marker seen on a legacy (non-exec) read: drain trailing ack.
                marker_seen_at = time.time()

        return b"".join(chunks).decode(errors="replace")

    @staticmethod
    def _has_first_line_terminator(text: str) -> bool:
        first = text.split("\n", 1)[0].strip()
        if first.startswith("OK") or first.startswith("FAIL") or first.startswith("ERROR"):
            # "OK <pid>" from spawn, bare "OK" from attach, FAIL/ERROR lines
            return first == "OK" or first.startswith(("OK ", "FAIL", "ERROR"))
        return False

    def _send(self, cmd: str) -> None:
        self.connect()
        assert self._sock is not None
        payload = cmd.rstrip("\n").encode() + b"\n"
        self._sock.sendall(payload)

    def _raw_command(self, cmd: str, **read_kwargs) -> str:
        self._send(cmd)
        return self._read_response(**read_kwargs)

    def _resync_agent(self, total_cap: float = 15.0) -> None:
        """Consume late/stale agent output with a harmless wrapped no-op exec."""
        if not self.connected():
            self._needs_resync = False
            return
        nonce = os.urandom(4).hex()
        try:
            raw = self._raw_command(
                "exec " + self.wrap_lua("", nonce),
                end_marker=f"[{nonce}]",
                stall_seconds=5.0,
                total_cap=total_cap,
            )
            self._needs_resync = f"[{nonce}]" not in raw
        except (RenefError, OSError):
            # Do not let a failed resync turn every later command into a wait.
            self._needs_resync = False

    def _command(
        self,
        cmd: str,
        quiet_seconds: float = 0.3,
        total_cap: float = 30.0,
        first_line_fast: bool = False,
        end_marker: Optional[str] = None,
        stall_seconds: Optional[float] = None,
        resync: bool = False,
    ) -> str:
        self.connect()
        if resync and self._needs_resync:
            self._resync_agent()
        return self._raw_command(
            cmd,
            quiet_seconds=quiet_seconds,
            total_cap=total_cap,
            first_line_fast=first_line_fast,
            end_marker=end_marker,
            stall_seconds=stall_seconds,
        )

    # -- server-level commands ----------------------------------------------

    def ping(self) -> bool:
        """Probe the server without touching session state.

        A transient error must not call close(): ping shares the client with
        the live target, and close() would drop pid/target_label and make
        every subsequent tool report "No active target" even though the
        instrumented process is still running.
        """
        try:
            return "pong" in self._command("ping", quiet_seconds=0.2, total_cap=3.0,
                                           first_line_fast=True)
        except (RenefError, OSError):
            return False

    def resume(self) -> str:
        return self._command("resume", quiet_seconds=0.3, total_cap=10.0,
                             first_line_fast=True).strip()

    # -- session commands ----------------------------------------------------

    def attach(self, pid: int) -> int:
        resp = self._command(f"attach {int(pid)}",
                             quiet_seconds=0.4, total_cap=60.0, first_line_fast=True)
        first = resp.strip().splitlines()[0] if resp.strip() else ""
        if not first.startswith("OK"):
            self.close()
            raise RenefError(f"attach({pid}) failed: {first or 'no response from server'}")
        self.pid = int(pid)
        self.target_label = f"pid {pid}"
        self._needs_resync = False
        return int(pid)

    def spawn(self, package: str) -> int:
        package = validate_package(package)
        # --pause is the upstream gate: the main thread is ptrace-stopped after
        # the agent is injected.  The first exec (or renef_resume) resumes it.
        resp = self._command(f"spawn {package} --pause",
                             quiet_seconds=0.6, total_cap=90.0, first_line_fast=True)
        first = resp.strip().splitlines()[0] if resp.strip() else ""
        pid = 0
        if first.startswith("OK"):
            try:
                pid = int(first.split()[1])
            except (IndexError, ValueError):
                pid = 0
        if pid <= 0:
            self.close()
            raise RenefError(
                f"spawn({package!r}) failed: {first or 'no response from server'}. "
                "Check the package name (renef_list_apps) and that renef_server runs as root."
            )
        self.pid = pid
        self.target_label = package
        self._needs_resync = False
        return pid

    # -- exec / script ---------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def wrap_lua(user_lua: str, nonce: str = "") -> str:
        """Wrap user Lua so execution errors surface and the relay marker prints.

        The marker carries a per-exec nonce: a stale marker from a previous exec
        will not match the current wait, so it cannot be mistaken for this
        command's completion.
        """
        tail = _EXEC_WRAPPER_TAIL_TMPL.format(nonce=nonce)
        return _EXEC_WRAPPER_HEAD + user_lua + tail

    def exec(self, user_lua: str, total_cap: float = 30.0) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """Run Lua in the target. Returns (is_error, cleaned_text).

        Waits for this exec's nonce-bearing completion marker.  On timeout it
        returns an explicit error and deliberately does NOT re-send the script:
        a blind retry executed non-idempotent hooks twice.  The next command
        resyncs the agent stream so late output cannot poison it.
        """
        nonce = os.urandom(4).hex()
        marker = f"[{nonce}]"
        raw = self._command(
            "exec " + self.wrap_lua(user_lua, nonce),
            end_marker=marker,
            stall_seconds=6.0,
            total_cap=total_cap,
            resync=True,
        )
        if marker not in raw:
            self._needs_resync = True
            _, partial = self._interpret_exec(raw, nonce)
            detail = (
                "exec timed out: completion marker not seen "
                f"(overall limit {total_cap:g}s, ~6s silence limit). "
                "The script may still be running; it was not retried to avoid "
                "duplicate execution."
            )
            if partial.strip():
                detail += "\npartial output:\n" + partial
            return True, detail
        self._needs_resync = False
        return self._interpret_exec(raw, nonce)

    @staticmethod
    def _interpret_exec(raw: str, nonce: str = "") -> tuple[bool, str]:
        lines = raw.splitlines()
        cleaned = []
        is_error = MARK_ERR_PREFIX in raw or "ERROR: Lua execution failed" in raw
        for ln in lines:
            s = ln.strip()
            if s == "OK" or s.startswith(MARK_OK):
                # completion marker (this exec's or stale) and agent ack
                continue
            if s.startswith(MARK_ERR_PREFIX):
                is_error = True
                # keep the error detail, drop the marker token itself
                cleaned.append(s[len(MARK_ERR_PREFIX) + 1:] if len(s) > len(MARK_ERR_PREFIX) else s)
                continue
            cleaned.append(ln)
        text = "\n".join(cleaned).strip("\n")
        return is_error, text

    def load_script(self, path: str, total_cap: float = 60.0) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        """Read a Lua file on this machine and run it in the target.

        Note: the on-device server's script command is "l <device_path>" and reads
        files from the DEVICE filesystem; we instead read locally and exec the
        contents, which is equivalent and needs no adb push.
        """
        p = os.path.expanduser(path)
        if not os.path.isfile(p):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"script not found: {p}")
        with open(p, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            script = f.read()
        if len(script) > 900_000:
            raise RenefError("script too large (>900KB)")
        return self.exec(script, total_cap=total_cap)

    # -- memory / hooks --------------------------------------------------------

    def memscan(self, pattern: str, module: str = "") -> str:
        """Scan process memory for a hex pattern.

        With ``module`` this uses Lua ``Memory.search`` (wildcards + spaces
        supported).  Without it, the upstream ``ms`` command is used; that path
        only accepts compact hex, has a hard ~10s wait, and is therefore not
        suitable for large/full scans.
        """
        module = module.strip()
        if module:
            normalized = normalize_hex_pattern(pattern, wildcards=True)
            lua = (
                f"local r = Memory.search({quote(normalized)}, {quote(module)}); "
                "if not r or #r == 0 then print('No matches found.') else "
                "print('Found ' .. #r .. ' match(es):'); "
                "for i, x in ipairs(r) do "
                "print(string.format('[%d] %s + 0x%x (addr: 0x%x)', i, "
                "tostring(x.library), x.offset, x.addr)); "
                "print('    Hex:   ' .. tostring(x.hex)); "
                "print('    ASCII: ' .. tostring(x.ascii)) end end"
            )
            is_err, text = self.exec(lua, total_cap=45.0)
            if is_err:
                raise RenefError(f"module-scoped scan failed: {text}")
            return text

        compact = normalize_hex_pattern(pattern, wildcards=False).replace(" ", "")
        raw = self._command("ms " + compact, quiet_seconds=0.5, total_cap=12.0,
                            resync=True)
        text = raw.strip()
        clean = text.lstrip().lower()
        # The upstream formatter can return partial JSON (which it reports as
        # "Error parsing response") or a JSON error object formatted as
        # "Error: ...". Anything that is not a complete formatted result can
        # leave late bytes queued in the agent socket -> resync next command.
        complete = (text.startswith("No matches found.")
                    or (clean.startswith("found ")
                        and "match(es):" in clean
                        and text.rstrip().endswith(("-" * 60))))
        if not text or "No response from agent" in text or not complete:
            self._needs_resync = True
        if not text or "No response from agent" in text:
            raise RenefError(
                "full-process scan exceeded the upstream server's ~10s response cap. "
                "The scan may still be running; its late output will be discarded on "
                "the next command. Re-run with module='libfoo.so' for a reliable "
                "module-scoped scan (supports spaces and ?? wildcards)."
            )
        if clean.startswith("error") or "error parsing" in clean:
            raise RenefError(f"memscan failed: {text}")
        return text

    def hooks(self) -> str:
        # Upstream `hooks` performs a single recv(). If a hook fires at the same
        # moment, that print is returned instead of the listing while the real
        # listing stays queued in the agent socket. Just send `hooks` again:
        # the queued listing surfaces on a later attempt. Resyncing between
        # attempts would DISCARD the queued listing, so only resync when a
        # previous timeout already flagged stale data.
        raw = ""
        for attempt in range(6):
            raw = self._command("hooks", quiet_seconds=0.6, total_cap=15.0,
                                first_line_fast=False, resync=(attempt == 0))
            idx = raw.find("Active hooks")
            if idx >= 0:
                return raw[idx:].strip()
            if raw.lstrip().upper().startswith("ERROR"):
                return raw.strip()
            if attempt < 5:
                time.sleep(0.3)
        # give up: queue drain on the next command so stale listings cannot
        # poison it
        self._needs_resync = True
        return raw.strip()

    def unhook(self, hook_id) -> str:
        return self._command(f"unhook {hook_id}", quiet_seconds=0.4, total_cap=15.0,
                             first_line_fast=False, resync=True).strip()

    # -- raw server commands (ELF sections, hook template generator) -----------

    @staticmethod
    def _safe_token(value: str, what: str) -> str:
        """Validate a single CLI token (lib/symbol names) before interpolation."""
        value = str(value).strip()
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._+/=\-]+", value):
            raise ValueError(
                f"{what} must be a plain name/path token (letters, digits, . _ + / = -), "
                f"got {value!r}"
            )
        return value

    def sections(self, lib: str) -> str:
        """Upstream `sec <lib>` server command: ELF sections of a loaded library."""
        lib = self._safe_token(lib, "lib")
        raw = self._command(f"sec {lib}", quiet_seconds=0.5, total_cap=15.0, resync=True)
        return raw.strip()

    def hookgen(self, lib: str, symbol_or_offset: str = "") -> str:
        """Upstream `hookgen` server command: generate a Lua hook template.

        hookgen(lib, offset) and hookgen(lib, symbol) need a target session;
        hookgen(symbol) searches common system libraries.
        """
        lib = self._safe_token(lib, "lib")
        cmd = f"hookgen {lib}"
        if symbol_or_offset.strip():
            cmd += " " + self._safe_token(symbol_or_offset, "symbol_or_offset")
        raw = self._command(cmd, quiet_seconds=1.0, total_cap=30.0, resync=True)
        return raw.strip()

    # -- watch ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def watch_collect(self, seconds: float, on_chunk=None) -> str:
        """Enter watch mode, collect hook output for `seconds`, then leave cleanly."""
        self.connect()
        if self._needs_resync:
            self._resync_agent()
        assert self._sock is not None
        sock = self._sock
        sock.settimeout(0.1)
        sock.sendall(b"watch\n")
        collected: list[bytes] = []
        deadline = time.time() + max(0.5, seconds)
        # read stream; hook output arrives while we hold the window open
        while time.time() < deadline:
            try:
                data = sock.recv(65536)
                if not data:
                    break
                collected.append(data)
                if on_chunk:
                    on_chunk(data)
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except OSError:
                break
        # exit watch: any byte makes the server exit watch mode
        try:
            sock.sendall(b"\n")
        except OSError:
            pass
        # drain the watch-exit + bare-command response (discarded)
        drain_deadline = time.time() + 2.0
        quiet_since: Optional[float] = None
        while time.time() < drain_deadline:
            try:
                data = sock.recv(65536)
                if not data:
                    break
                quiet_since = None
            except socket.timeout:
                if quiet_since is None:
                    quiet_since = time.time()
                elif time.time() - quiet_since >= 0.4:
                    break
            except OSError:
                break
        text = b"".join(collected).decode(errors="replace")
        # Filter: the watch banner, and stale exec-leftover lines (bare "OK" or
        # "✓ Lua executed" lines forwarded from the agent socket buffer by the
        # server's watch-mode initial drain).
        return "\n".join(
            ln for ln in text.splitlines()
            if ln.strip() and ln.strip() != "OK"
            and "✓ Lua executed" not in ln
            and not ln.startswith("Watching hook output")
        ).strip("\n")

    # -- module helpers (implemented over exec, correct Lua) --------------------

    def module_list(self) -> list[dict]:
        """Parse Module.list() maps-style output: first mapping per unique .so path."""
        is_err, text = self.exec(
            "local m = Module.list(); if m then print(m) else print('__NIL__') end",
            total_cap=60.0,
        )
        if is_err or "__NIL__" in text:
            if is_err:
                raise RenefError(f"Module.list failed: {text}")
            return []
        mods: list[dict] = []
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for ln in text.splitlines():
            ln = ln.strip()
            if not ln or "-" not in ln:
                continue
            head, _, path = ln.partition(" ")
            path = path.strip()
            if not path or not path.endswith(".so") or path in seen:
                continue
            start_s = head.split("-", 1)[0]
            try:
                base = int(start_s, 16)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            seen.add(path)
            mods.append({"base": base, "name": os.path.basename(path), "path": path})
        mods.sort(key=lambda m: m["base"])
        return mods

    def module_find(self, name: str) -> Optional[int]:
        is_err, text = self.exec(
            f"local a = Module.find({quote(name)}); "
            "if a then print(string.format('%d', a)) else print('__NIL__') end",
            total_cap=30.0,
        )
        if is_err:
            raise RenefError(f"Module.find failed: {text}")
        val = text.strip().splitlines()[-1] if text.strip() else ""
        if not val or "__NIL__" in val:
            return None
        try:
            return int(val)
        except ValueError:
            return None

    def _module_symbols_raw(self, fn: str, name: str) -> str:
        lua = (
            f"local s = Module.{fn}({quote(name)}); "
            "if not s then print('__NIL__') else "
            "for _, x in ipairs(s) do print(string.format('0x%x %s', x.offset, x.name)) end end"
        )
        is_err, text = self.exec(lua, total_cap=60.0)
        if is_err:
            raise RenefError(f"Module.{fn} failed: {text}")
        return text

    def module_exports(self, name: str) -> list[dict]:
        text = self._module_symbols_raw("exports", name)
        if "__NIL__" in text:
            return []
        return self._parse_symbol_lines(text)

    def module_symbols(self, name: str) -> list[dict]:
        text = self._module_symbols_raw("symbols", name)
        if "__NIL__" in text:
            return []
        return self._parse_symbol_lines(text)

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_symbol_lines(text: str) -> list[dict]:
        items = []
        for ln in text.splitlines():
            ln = ln.strip()
            if not ln.startswith("0x"):
                continue
            off_s, _, sym = ln.partition(" ")
            try:
                items.append({"offset": int(off_s, 16), "name": sym.strip()})
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return items

```

`renef_mcp/config.py`:

```py
"""Path/env configuration for renef-mcp.

Environment variables:
    RENEF_HOME   Renef repo root (default: ~/github/renef)
    RENEF_HOST   renef_server host (default: 127.0.0.1, via adb forward)
    RENEF_PORT   renef_server TCP port (default: 1907)
    RENEF_ADB    adb binary path (default: "adb")
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
DEFAULT_PORT = 1907
# Abstract UDS socket the on-device server listens on (see renef Makefile install target)
FORWARD_REMOTE = "localabstract:com.android.internal.os.RuntimeInit"


def renef_home() -> Path:
    """Renef repo root containing build/librenef.so, bindings/python and RENEF_AI_PROMPT.md."""
    env = os.environ.get("RENEF_HOME", "").strip()
    if env:
        return Path(env).expanduser().resolve()
    return (Path.home() / "github" / "renef").resolve()


def binding_python_dir() -> Path:
    return renef_home() / "bindings" / "python"


def binding_core_py() -> Path:
    return binding_python_dir() / "renef" / "core.py"


def native_lib_path() -> Path:
    return renef_home() / "build" / "librenef.so"


def lua_api_doc_path() -> Path:
    return renef_home() / "RENEF_AI_PROMPT.md"


def adb_binary() -> str:
    return os.environ.get("RENEF_ADB", "adb").strip() or "adb"


def host() -> str:
    return os.environ.get("RENEF_HOST", DEFAULT_HOST).strip() or DEFAULT_HOST


def port() -> int:
    raw = os.environ.get("RENEF_PORT", "").strip()
    try:
        return int(raw) if raw else DEFAULT_PORT
    except ValueError:
        return DEFAULT_PORT


def uses_local_forward() -> bool:
    """True when the server is reached through a local adb port forward."""
    h = host()
    return h in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost", "::1", "0.0.0.0")

```

`renef_mcp/lua.py`:

```py
"""Small Lua helpers shared by the MCP tools and the TCP client.

Kept dependency-free: the values interpolated into Lua are host-controlled
module names, class names, and hook selectors, never raw user Lua.  The goal is
to make the generated Lua a valid string literal regardless of quotes,
backslashes, newlines, or non-ASCII bytes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re


def quote(value: str) -> str:
    """Return a double-quoted Lua string literal for *value*."""
    out = ['"']
    for ch in str(value):
        code = ord(ch)
        if ch == "\\":
            out.append("\\\\")
        elif ch == '"':
            out.append('\\"')
        elif ch == "\n":
            out.append("\\n")
        elif ch == "\r":
            out.append("\\r")
        elif ch == "\t":
            out.append("\\t")
        elif code < 0x20 or code == 0x7F:
            out.append(f"\\{code:03d}")
        else:
            out.append(ch)
    out.append('"')
    return "".join(out)


_HEX_TOKEN = re.compile(r"^(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{2}|\?\?)$")


def normalize_hex_pattern(pattern: str, *, wildcards: bool) -> str:
    """Normalize a user hex pattern to space-separated byte tokens.

    Accepts compact (``7f454c46``), spaced (``7F 45 4C 46``), ``0x``-prefixed
    tokens, and (when *wildcards* is true) ``??`` tokens.  Raises ValueError on
    anything else instead of silently sending a corrupted pattern upstream.
    """
    raw = str(pattern).strip().lower()
    if not raw:
        raise ValueError("pattern must not be empty")

    # Drop a single leading 0x, and tolerate 0x on individual spaced tokens.
    if raw.startswith("0x"):
        raw = raw[2:]
    raw = re.sub(r"\b0x", "", raw)
    raw = raw.replace(",", " ").replace(":", " ")

    tokens: list[str] = []
    for chunk in raw.split():
        if len(chunk) <= 2:
            tokens.append(chunk)
            continue
        if len(chunk) % 2 != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"hex pattern chunk {chunk!r} has an odd number of digits "
                "(expected pairs such as 7f45 or 7f 45)"
            )
        tokens.extend(chunk[i : i + 2] for i in range(0, len(chunk), 2))

    if not tokens:
        raise ValueError("pattern must contain at least one byte")
    for token in tokens:
        if token == "??":
            if not wildcards:
                raise ValueError(
                    "?? wildcards are not supported by the upstream full-memory "
                    "scan; pass module=... to use the Lua Memory.search backend"
                )
            continue
        if not _HEX_TOKEN.fullmatch(token):
            raise ValueError(
                f"invalid hex byte {token!r}; use two hex digits per byte or ??"
            )
    return " ".join(tokens)

```

`renef_mcp/scripts.py`:

```py
"""Vendored Lua script library for renef-mcp.

Scripts come from ahmeth4n/renef-mcp (MIT) and are small, self-contained Renef
Lua examples: hook demos, hexdump, syscall tracing, and ready-made bypasses
(flutter_ssl, ssl_unpin, root_bypass).  They are exposed to agents through the
renef_list_scripts / renef_get_script tools, mirroring the frida script library
pattern in android-re-mcp.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

_SCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "scripts"


def list_scripts() -> list[str]:
    """Names (without .lua) of all bundled scripts, sorted."""
    return sorted(p.stem for p in _SCRIPT_DIR.glob("*.lua"))


def get_script_content(name: str) -> str:
    """Return the Lua source of a bundled script. Raises FileNotFoundError."""
    safe = Path(name).stem  # reject paths/subdirs
    path = _SCRIPT_DIR / f"{safe}.lua"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no bundled script named {name!r}")
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

```

`renef_mcp/scripts/dere_0x9.lua`:

```lua
-- Hook liba0x9.so first export and return 1337

local lib_name = "liba0x9.so"

print("Looking for " .. lib_name .. "...")

-- Check if library is already loaded
if not Module.find(lib_name) then
    print("[WARN] Library not loaded yet")
    print("[INFO] Trigger the function in the app first, then reload this script")
    return
end

print("Library found! Getting exports...")

local exports = Module.exports(lib_name)
if not exports or #exports == 0 then
    print("[ERROR] No exports in " .. lib_name)
    return
end

-- Hook first export
local func = exports[1]
print("Hooking: " .. func.name .. " @ 0x" .. string.format("%x", func.offset))

hook(lib_name, func.offset, {
    onEnter = function(args)
        print("[CALLED] " .. func.name)
    end,
    onLeave = function(retval)
        print("Original retval: " .. retval)
        return 1337
    end
})

print("Hook installed! Return value will be 1337")

`renef_mcp/scripts/flutter_ssl.lua`:

```lua
-- Flutter SSL Pinning Bypass for RENEF
-- Works for Flutter apps using BoringSSL

print("[*] Flutter SSL Pinning Bypass loading...")

-- Hardcoded offset for ssl_crypto_x509_session_verify_cert_chain
-- This offset may vary per Flutter version - update if needed
local SSL_VERIFY_OFFSET = 0x5dc730

local bypass_installed = false

-- Function to install SSL bypass on libflutter.so
local function install_ssl_bypass()
    if bypass_installed then
        return true
    end

    local flutter_base = Module.find("libflutter.so")
    if not flutter_base then
        return false
    end

    print(string.format("[+] libflutter.so found at: 0x%x", flutter_base))
    print(string.format("[+] Installing hook at offset: 0x%x", SSL_VERIFY_OFFSET))

    -- Install hook to bypass SSL verification
    hook("libflutter.so", SSL_VERIFY_OFFSET, {
        onEnter = function(args)
            print("[*] SSL verify called!")
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print("[*] SSL verify bypassing, returning 1")
            return 1  -- Return success (1 = verified)
        end
    })

    bypass_installed = true
    print("[+] SSL pinning bypass ACTIVE!")
    return true
end

-- Try to install bypass immediately if libflutter is already loaded
if install_ssl_bypass() then
    print("[+] Bypass installed on existing libflutter.so")
else
    print("[*] libflutter.so not loaded yet, hooking linker...")

    -- Hook android_dlopen_ext which is used to load libraries
    local linker_name = "linker64"
    local linker_base = Module.find(linker_name)

    if not linker_base then
        print("[-] linker64 not found, trying linker")
        linker_name = "linker"
        linker_base = Module.find(linker_name)
    end

    if not linker_base then
        print("[-] Cannot find linker!")
    else
        print(string.format("[+] %s found at: 0x%x", linker_name, linker_base))

        -- Get linker symbols
        local linker_symbols = Module.symbols(linker_name)
        if not linker_symbols then
            print("[-] Cannot get linker symbols (may be stripped)")
            print("[*] Trying exports instead...")
            linker_symbols = Module.exports(linker_name)
        end

        if linker_symbols then
            -- Find __dl__Z9do_dlopenPKciPK17android_dlextinfoPKv or similar
            local dlopen_offset = nil
            for _, sym in ipairs(linker_symbols) do
                if sym.name:find("do_dlopen") then
                    dlopen_offset = sym.offset
                    print(string.format("[+] Found %s at offset 0x%x", sym.name, sym.offset))
                    break
                end
            end

            if dlopen_offset then
                print(string.format("[+] Hooking %s + 0x%x", linker_name, dlopen_offset))

                hook(linker_name, dlopen_offset, {
                    onEnter = function(args)
                        -- args[0] is the library path
                        local path = Memory.readString(args[0])
                        if path and path:find("libflutter") then
                            print("[+] libflutter.so loading: " .. path)
                        end
                    end,
                    onLeave = function(retval)
                        -- After library loads, try to install bypass
                        if not bypass_installed then
                            install_ssl_bypass()
                        end
                    end
                })
                print("[+] Linker hook installed, waiting for libflutter.so...")
            else
                print("[-] do_dlopen not found in linker symbols")
            end
        else
            print("[-] Cannot get linker symbols/exports")
        end
    end
end

print("[+] Flutter SSL Bypass script loaded")

```

`renef_mcp/scripts/java_api_example.lua`:

```lua
-- Java API Test Script
-- Usage: spawn com.example.app && l scripts/test_java_api.lua

print("=== Java API Test ===")

-- Test 1: Load class with Java.use 
print("\n[1] Testing Java.use()...")
local Build = Java.use("android/os/Build")
if Build then
    print("  OK: android.os.Build loaded")
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load Build class")
    return
end

-- Test 2: Static method (non-argument)
print("\n[2] Testing static method call...")
local System = Java.use("java/lang/System")
if System then
    local time = System:call("currentTimeMillis", "()J")
    print(string.format("  System.currentTimeMillis() = %d", time or 0))
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load System class")
end

-- Test 3: Static method (with argument)
print("\n[3] Testing static method with args...")
local String = Java.use("java/lang/String")
if String then
    local str = String:call("valueOf", "(I)Ljava/lang/String;", 123)
    print(string.format("  String.valueOf(123) = %s", tostring(str)))

    local str2 = String:call("valueOf", "(Z)Ljava/lang/String;", true)
    print(string.format("  String.valueOf(true) = %s", tostring(str2)))
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load String class")
end

-- Test 4: Instance creation + instance method
print("\n[4] Testing instance creation and methods...")
local StringBuilder = Java.use("java/lang/StringBuilder")
if StringBuilder then
    local sb = StringBuilder:new("()V")
    if sb then
        print("  OK: StringBuilder instance created")

        sb:call("append", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/StringBuilder;", "Hello")
        sb:call("append", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/StringBuilder;", " World")
        local result = sb:call("toString", "()Ljava/lang/String;")
        print(string.format("  StringBuilder result: %s", tostring(result)))
    else
        print("  FAIL: Could not create StringBuilder instance")
    end
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load StringBuilder class")
end

-- Test 5: Android Context
print("\n[5] Testing Android APIs...")
local ActivityThread = Java.use("android/app/ActivityThread")
if ActivityThread then
    local app = ActivityThread:call("currentApplication", "()Landroid/app/Application;")
    if app then
        local pkgName = app:call("getPackageName", "()Ljava/lang/String;")
        print(string.format("  Package: %s", tostring(pkgName)))
    else
        print("  INFO: No Application context")
    end
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load ActivityThread")
end

print("\n=== Test Complete ===")

```

`renef_mcp/scripts/java_hook.lua`:

```lua
-- Test script for Java String return values
-- Target: com.example.reneftestapp

print("[*] Starting Java String hook test...")

-- Hook childHook - modify return value
hook("com/example/reneftestapp/MainActivity", "childHook", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/String;", {
    onEnter = function(args)
        print("[+] childHook called")
        print("    this = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[1]))
        print("    param (jstring ref) = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[2]))
    end,
    onLeave = function(retval)
        print("[+] childHook onLeave")
        print("    Original: \"" .. (retval.value or "nil") .. "\"")

        -- Modify the return value!
        local newValue = "HOOKED BY RENEF!"
        print("    Replacing with: \"" .. newValue .. "\"")

        return JNI.string(newValue)
    end
})

-- Hook parentHook - just observe
hook("com/example/reneftestapp/MainActivity", "parentHook", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/String;", {
    onEnter = function(args)
        print("[+] parentHook called")
        print("    this = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[1]))
        print("    param (jstring ref) = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[2]))
    end,
    onLeave = function(retval)
        print("[+] parentHook onLeave")
        print("    Return (raw ref): " .. string.format("0x%x", retval.raw))
        if retval.value then
            print("    Return (string): \"" .. retval.value .. "\"")
        end
        return retval.raw
    end
})

print("[+] Hooks installed!")
print("[*] childHook return value will be replaced with 'HOOKED BY RENEF!'")

```

`renef_mcp/scripts/kcov_test.lua`:

```lua
-- KCov Test Script
-- After injecting into Renef: l scripts/examples/kcov_test.lua
--
-- Requirement: kernel CONFIG_KCOV=y
-- Check: adb shell "ls /sys/kernel/debug/kcov"

print(CYAN .. "=== KCov Test ===" .. RESET)

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 1: Can it be opened?
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[1] KCov.open() test...")

local ok, cov = pcall(KCov.open)
if not ok then
    print(RED .. "  FAIL: " .. tostring(cov) .. RESET)
    print(RED .. "  Kernel does not support CONFIG_KCOV=y." .. RESET)
    print(YELLOW .. "  Check: adb shell ls /sys/kernel/debug/kcov" .. RESET)
    return
end
print(GREEN .. "  OK: KCov opened" .. RESET)

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 2: enable/disable cycle
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[2] enable/disable test...")

cov:reset()
cov:enable()

-- make a simple syscall to trigger kernel paths
local f = io.open("/proc/self/maps", "r")
if f then
    local _ = f:read(100)
    f:close()
end

cov:disable()

local count = cov:count()
print(string.format("  Hit: %d kernel functions", count))

if count > 0 then
    print(GREEN .. "  OK: Coverage recorded" .. RESET)
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  WARN: 0 hits - KCOV may not be active" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 3: collect() - PC addresses
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[3] collect() test...")

local pcs = cov:collect(20) -- first 20 entries
print(string.format("  Total: %d PCs, showing first %d:", count, #pcs))

for i, pc in ipairs(pcs) do
    print(string.format("    [%2d] 0x%x", i, pc))
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 4: reset + new measurement
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[4] reset + new measurement test...")

cov:reset()
assert(cov:count() == 0, "count should be 0 after reset")
print("  reset OK (count=0)")

cov:enable()

-- different syscall
os.time()

cov:disable()

local count2 = cov:count()
print(string.format("  New measurement: %d hits", count2))

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 5: edges() - coverage-guided fuzzing
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[5] edges() test...")

-- measure baseline
cov:reset()
cov:enable()
local f1 = io.open("/proc/self/status", "r")
if f1 then f1:read("*a"); f1:close() end
cov:disable()

local edges1 = cov:edges()
local edge_count = 0
for _ in pairs(edges1) do edge_count = edge_count + 1 end
print(string.format("  Baseline: %d unique edges", edge_count))

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 6: diff() - new path detection
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[6] diff() test...")

-- try a different path
cov:reset()
cov:enable()
local f2 = io.open("/proc/self/cmdline", "r")
if f2 then f2:read("*a"); f2:close() end
cov:disable()

local new_edges = cov:diff(edges1)
print(string.format("  New edges: %d", new_edges))

if new_edges > 0 then
    print(GREEN .. "  OK: Different syscall = different kernel path" .. RESET)
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  INFO: Same path may have been used" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 7: syscall mutation + kcov combined
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[7] Syscall mutation + KCov combined test...")

-- This test uses kcov to compare coverage across different inputs
-- Miniature version of a real fuzzing loop

local results = {}

-- try 3 different mutated inputs
local test_fds = {"/proc/self/maps", "/proc/self/status", "/proc/self/stat"}

for i, path in ipairs(test_fds) do
    cov:reset()
    cov:enable()

    local fh = io.open(path, "r")
    if fh then fh:read("*a"); fh:close() end

    cov:disable()

    local c = cov:count()
    local e = cov:edges()
    local ne = 0
    for _ in pairs(e) do ne = ne + 1 end

    results[i] = {path=path, hits=c, edges=ne}
    print(string.format("  Input %d: %s -> %d hits, %d edges", i, path, c, ne))
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Cleanup
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[8] Cleanup...")
cov:close()
print(GREEN .. "  KCov closed" .. RESET)

-----------------------------------------------
-- Summary
-----------------------------------------------
print(CYAN .. "\n=== Test Complete ===" .. RESET)
print(string.format("  Test 1 (open):    %s", cov and "PASS" or "FAIL"))
print(string.format("  Test 2 (enable):  %s", count > 0 and "PASS" or "WARN"))
print(string.format("  Test 3 (collect): %s", #pcs > 0 and "PASS" or "WARN"))
print(string.format("  Test 5 (edges):   %s", edge_count > 0 and "PASS" or "WARN"))

`renef_mcp/scripts/root_bypass.lua`:

```lua
-- ============================================================
-- Root Detection Bypass Script for Android
-- ============================================================
-- This script hooks common root detection methods and bypasses them
-- by intercepting system calls and returning false positives
-- ============================================================

-- Set hook type to trampoline (more reliable for system calls)
__hook_type__ = "trampoline"

-- Color constants for better logging
local RED = "\27[31m"
local GREEN = "\27[32m"
local YELLOW = "\27[33m"
local BLUE = "\27[34m"
local CYAN = "\27[36m"
local MAGENTA = "\27[35m"
local RESET = "\27[0m"

print(CYAN .. "+========================================================+" .. RESET)
print(CYAN .. "|      Root Detection Bypass Script v1.0                |" .. RESET)
print(CYAN .. "|      Multi-layer Anti-Root Detection                  |" .. RESET)
print(CYAN .. "+========================================================+" .. RESET)
print("")

-- ============================================================
-- Configuration
-- ============================================================
local config = {
    -- Root detection file paths to hide
    root_files = {
        "/system/app/Superuser.apk",
        "/sbin/su",
        "/system/bin/su",
        "/system/xbin/su",
        "/data/local/xbin/su",
        "/data/local/bin/su",
        "/system/sd/xbin/su",
        "/system/bin/failsafe/su",
        "/data/local/su",
        "/su/bin/su",
        "/system/xbin/daemonsu",
        "/system/etc/init.d/99SuperSUDaemon",
        "/dev/com.koushikdutta.superuser.daemon/",
        "/system/app/SuperSU",
        "/system/xbin/sugote",
        "/sbin/magisk",
        "/sbin/magiskhide",
        "/system/xbin/magisk",
        "/data/adb/magisk"
    },

    -- Root detection package names
    root_packages = {
        "com.noshufou.android.su",
        "com.thirdparty.superuser",
        "eu.chainfire.supersu",
        "com.koushikdutta.superuser",
        "com.zachspong.temprootremovejb",
        "com.ramdroid.appquarantine",
        "com.topjohnwu.magisk"
    },

    -- Dangerous properties to spoof
    dangerous_props = {
        "ro.debuggable",
        "ro.secure",
        "ro.build.tags",
        "ro.build.type"
    },

    -- Enable/disable specific bypasses
    enable_file_access_bypass = true,
    enable_exec_bypass = true,
    enable_package_manager_bypass = true,
    enable_system_properties_bypass = true,
    enable_selinux_bypass = true,

    -- Logging
    verbose_logging = true
}

-- ============================================================
-- Helper Functions
-- ============================================================

local function log_info(msg)
    if config.verbose_logging then
        print(BLUE .. "[INFO] " .. RESET .. msg)
    end
end

local function log_success(msg)
    print(GREEN .. "[OK] " .. RESET .. msg)
end

local function log_hook(func_name, lib_name)
    print(YELLOW .. "[HOOK] " .. RESET .. func_name .. MAGENTA .. " @ " .. RESET .. lib_name)
end

local function log_intercept(func_name, action)
    if config.verbose_logging then
        print(CYAN .. "[INTERCEPT] " .. RESET .. func_name .. " -> " .. action)
    end
end

local function is_root_path(path)
    if not path then return false end
    for _, root_file in ipairs(config.root_files) do
        if string.find(path, root_file, 1, true) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function is_root_package(pkg_name)
    if not pkg_name then return false end
    for _, root_pkg in ipairs(config.root_packages) do
        if string.find(pkg_name, root_pkg, 1, true) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

-- ============================================================
-- libc Hooks - File Access Bypass
-- ============================================================

local function setup_file_access_hooks()
    if not config.enable_file_access_bypass then
        return
    end

    print("")
    print(GREEN .. "=== File Access Hooks ===" .. RESET)

    local libc_base = Module.find("libc.so")
    if not libc_base then
        print(RED .. "[ERROR] Could not find libc.so" .. RESET)
        return
    end

    log_info("libc.so base: " .. string.format("0x%x", libc_base))

    -- Get libc exports
    local exports = Module.exports("libc.so")
    if not exports then
        print(RED .. "[ERROR] Could not get libc exports" .. RESET)
        return
    end

    -- Hook open()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "open" then
            log_hook("open()", "libc.so")
            local current_path = nil
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    -- args[0] is the file path pointer
                    local success, path = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and path then
                        current_path = path
                        if is_root_path(path) then
                            log_intercept("open", "BLOCKED: " .. path)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return -1 (ENOENT) for root files
                    if current_path and is_root_path(current_path) then
                        current_path = nil
                        return -1
                    end
                    current_path = nil
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked open()")
            break
        end
    end

    -- Hook access()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "access" then
            log_hook("access()", "libc.so")
            local current_path = nil
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    local success, path = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and path then
                        current_path = path
                        if is_root_path(path) then
                            log_intercept("access", "BLOCKED: " .. path)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return -1 for root file access checks
                    if current_path and is_root_path(current_path) then
                        current_path = nil
                        return -1
                    end
                    current_path = nil
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked access()")
            break
        end
    end

    -- Hook stat()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "stat" or export.name == "__xstat" then
            log_hook("stat()", "libc.so")
            local current_path = nil
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    -- For stat, first arg might be version for __xstat
                    local path_arg = (export.name == "__xstat") and args[1] or args[0]
                    local success, path = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(path_arg) end)
                    if success and path then
                        current_path = path
                        if is_root_path(path) then
                            log_intercept("stat", "BLOCKED: " .. path)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return -1 for root file stat checks
                    if current_path and is_root_path(current_path) then
                        current_path = nil
                        return -1
                    end
                    current_path = nil
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked stat()")
            break
        end
    end

    -- Hook fopen()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "fopen" then
            log_hook("fopen()", "libc.so")
            local current_path = nil
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    local success, path = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and path then
                        current_path = path
                        -- Always log to test watch mode
                        print(CYAN .. "[FOPEN] " .. RESET .. path)
                        if is_root_path(path) then
                            log_intercept("fopen", "BLOCKED: " .. path)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return NULL for root files
                    if current_path and is_root_path(current_path) then
                        current_path = nil
                        return 0
                    end
                    current_path = nil
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked fopen()")
            break
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- Process Execution Bypass
-- ============================================================

local function setup_exec_hooks()
    if not config.enable_exec_bypass then
        return
    end

    print("")
    print(GREEN .. "=== Process Execution Hooks ===" .. RESET)

    local exports = Module.exports("libc.so")
    if not exports then
        return
    end

    -- Hook system()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "system" then
            log_hook("system()", "libc.so")
            local is_su_command = false
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    local success, cmd = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and cmd then
                        if string.find(cmd, "su", 1, true) or string.find(cmd, "which su", 1, true) then
                            is_su_command = true
                            log_intercept("system", "BLOCKED: " .. cmd)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return non-zero (failure) for su commands
                    if is_su_command then
                        is_su_command = false
                        return 127  -- Command not found
                    end
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked system()")
            break
        end
    end

    -- Hook popen()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "popen" then
            log_hook("popen()", "libc.so")
            local is_su_command = false
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    local success, cmd = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and cmd then
                        if string.find(cmd, "su", 1, true) or string.find(cmd, "which su", 1, true) then
                            is_su_command = true
                            log_intercept("popen", "BLOCKED: " .. cmd)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return NULL for su/which su commands
                    if is_su_command then
                        is_su_command = false
                        return 0
                    end
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked popen()")
            break
        end
    end

    -- Hook execve()
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "execve" then
            log_hook("execve()", "libc.so")
            local is_su_exec = false
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    local success, path = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and path then
                        if is_root_path(path) or string.find(path, "su", 1, true) then
                            is_su_exec = true
                            log_intercept("execve", "BLOCKED: " .. path)
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return -1 for su execution attempts
                    if is_su_exec then
                        is_su_exec = false
                        return -1
                    end
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked execve()")
            break
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- System Properties Bypass
-- ============================================================

local function setup_properties_hooks()
    if not config.enable_system_properties_bypass then
        return
    end

    print("")
    print(GREEN .. "=== System Properties Hooks ===" .. RESET)

    local libc_base = Module.find("libc.so")
    if not libc_base then
        return
    end

    local exports = Module.exports("libc.so")
    if not exports then
        return
    end

    -- Hook __system_property_get
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "__system_property_get" then
            log_hook("__system_property_get()", "libc.so")
            hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    -- args[0] is property name, args[1] is output buffer
                    local success, prop = pcall(function() return Memory.readString(args[0]) end)
                    if success and prop then
                        -- Spoof dangerous properties
                        if prop == "ro.debuggable" then
                            Memory.writeString(args[1], "0")
                            log_intercept("__system_property_get", "SPOOFED: ro.debuggable = 0")
                        elseif prop == "ro.secure" then
                            Memory.writeString(args[1], "1")
                            log_intercept("__system_property_get", "SPOOFED: ro.secure = 1")
                        elseif prop == "ro.build.tags" then
                            Memory.writeString(args[1], "release-keys")
                            log_intercept("__system_property_get", "SPOOFED: ro.build.tags = release-keys")
                        elseif prop == "ro.build.type" then
                            Memory.writeString(args[1], "user")
                            log_intercept("__system_property_get", "SPOOFED: ro.build.type = user")
                        end
                    end
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    return retval
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked __system_property_get()")
            break
        end
    end
end
