```lua
local function setup_selinux_hooks()
    if not config.enable_selinux_bypass then
        return
    end

    print("")
    print(GREEN .. "=== SELinux Hooks ===" .. RESET)

    local libselinux_base = Module.find("libselinux.so")
    if not libselinux_base then
        log_info("libselinux.so not found, skipping")
        return
    end

    log_info("libselinux.so base: " .. string.format("0x%x", libselinux_base))

    local exports = Module.exports("libselinux.so")
    if not exports then
        return
    end

    -- Hook is_selinux_enabled
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "is_selinux_enabled" then
            log_hook("is_selinux_enabled()", "libselinux.so")
            hook("libselinux.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    log_intercept("is_selinux_enabled", "returning enabled")
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return 1 (enabled) to look legitimate
                    return 1
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked is_selinux_enabled()")
            break
        end
    end

    -- Hook security_getenforce
    for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
        if export.name == "security_getenforce" then
            log_hook("security_getenforce()", "libselinux.so")
            hook("libselinux.so", export.offset, {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    log_intercept("security_getenforce", "returning enforcing")
                end,
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    -- Return 1 (enforcing mode)
                    return 1
                end
            })
            log_success("Hooked security_getenforce()")
            break
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- Additional Detection Methods
-- ============================================================

local function setup_additional_hooks()
    print("")
    print(GREEN .. "=== Additional Security Hooks ===" .. RESET)

    -- Hook getpwuid (used to check for su user)
    local exports = Module.exports("libc.so")
    if exports then
        for i, export in ipairs(exports) do
            if export.name == "getpwuid" then
                log_hook("getpwuid()", "libc.so")
                hook("libc.so", export.offset, {
                    onEnter = function(args)
                        -- args[0] is uid, 0 is root
                        local uid = tonumber(args[0])
                        if uid == 0 then
                            log_intercept("getpwuid", "BLOCKED: root uid (0)")
                        end
                    end,
                    onLeave = function(retval)
                        -- Return NULL for root uid checks
                        if tonumber(retval) ~= 0 then
                            local success, name = pcall(function()
                                local passwd_ptr = retval
                                local name_ptr = Memory.read(passwd_ptr, 8)  -- first field is pw_name
                                return Memory.readString(name_ptr)
                            end)
                            if success and name and (name == "root" or name == "su") then
                                log_intercept("getpwuid", "SPOOFED: hiding root user")
                                return 0
                            end
                        end
                        return retval
                    end
                })
                log_success("Hooked getpwuid()")
                break
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- Memory Scanning for Known Root Signatures
-- ============================================================

local function scan_for_signatures()
    print("")
    print(GREEN .. "=== Scanning for Root Signatures ===" .. RESET)

    -- Scan for common root-related strings in memory
    local signatures = {
        "Superuser",
        "SuperSU",
        "Magisk",
        "/system/xbin/su"
    }

    for _, sig in ipairs(signatures) do
        log_info("Scanning for: " .. sig)
        local results = Memory.scan(sig)

        if results and #results > 0 then
            print(YELLOW .. "[!] Found " .. #results .. " occurrence(s) of: " .. sig .. RESET)

            if config.verbose_logging then
                for i, r in ipairs(results) do
                    if i <= 3 then -- Limit output
                        print("    Library: " .. r.library)
                        print("    Offset:  " .. string.format("0x%x", r.offset))
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
-- Main Execution
-- ============================================================

print("")
print(YELLOW .. "Initializing bypass hooks..." .. RESET)

-- Debug: Check if libc.so is available
print(BLUE .. "[DEBUG] Checking for libc.so..." .. RESET)
local libc_test = Module.find("libc.so")

    print(GREEN .. "[DEBUG] libc.so found at: " .. string.format("0x%x", libc_test) .. RESET)

    if mods then
        for i, m in ipairs(mods) do
            if i <= 5 then
                print("  " .. m.name)
            end
        end
    end
end

-- Setup all hooks
setup_file_access_hooks()
setup_exec_hooks()
setup_properties_hooks()
setup_selinux_hooks()
setup_additional_hooks()

-- Optional: Scan for signatures
-- scan_for_signatures()

print(GREEN .. "+========================================================+" .. RESET)
print(GREEN .. "|      Root Bypass Script Successfully Loaded!          |" .. RESET)
print(GREEN .. "+========================================================+" .. RESET)
print("")
print(CYAN .. "Configuration:" .. RESET)
print("  File Access Bypass:     " .. (config.enable_file_access_bypass and GREEN .. "ON" or RED .. "OFF") .. RESET)
print("  Exec Bypass:            " .. (config.enable_exec_bypass and GREEN .. "ON" or RED .. "OFF") .. RESET)
```

```lua
-- Very simple fopen hook test
__hook_type__ = "trampoline"

print("=== Simple fopen Hook Test ===")

local libc = Module.find("libc.so")
if not libc then
    print("[ERROR] libc.so not found")
    return
end

print("[OK] libc.so @ " .. string.format("0x%x", libc))

local exports = Module.exports("libc.so")
if not exports then
    print("[ERROR] No exports")
    return
end

print("[OK] Got " .. #exports .. " exports")

-- Find fopen
for i, exp in ipairs(exports) do
    if exp.name == "fopen" then
        print("[FOUND] fopen @ offset: " .. string.format("0x%x", exp.offset))

        -- Hook it
        print("[INSTALL] Installing hook...")
        hook("libc.so", exp.offset, {
            onEnter = function(args)
                print(">>> FOPEN CALLED! <<<")
            end
        })
        print("[OK] Hook installed!")
        break
    end
end
```

`renef_mcp/scripts/ssl_unpin.lua`:

```lua
-- SSL Pinning Bypass for Renef
-- written by @ahmeth4n
-- Usage: ./renef -s com.example.app -l scripts/ssl_unpin.lua
--
-- Covers 30+ bypass targets:
--   Java: TrustManagerImpl, OkHttp3, OkHttp2 (Squareup), Trustkit,
--         Conscrypt, Apache, Appcelerator, Fabric, Netty, CWAC-Netsecurity,
--         IBM MobileFirst/WorkLight, PhoneGap, Chromium Cronet,
--         Flutter plugins, WebViewClient, Cordova, Boye
--   Native: OpenSSL/BoringSSL SSL_CTX_set_verify
--   Flutter: libflutter.so pattern-based + offset-based bypass
--
--
__hook_type__ = "trampoline"

print(CYAN .. "=== Renef SSL Pinning Bypass ===" .. RESET)

local bypass_count = 0

local function safe_hook(class, method, sig, callbacks, label)
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        hook(class, method, sig, callbacks)
    end)
    if ok then
        bypass_count = bypass_count + 1
        print(GREEN .. "  [+] " .. label .. RESET)
    else
        print(YELLOW .. "  [-] " .. label .. " (not found)" .. RESET)
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------
-- 1. Android TrustManagerImpl
--    [needs ExceptionClear for full bypass]
--
--    Hook arg layout (instance method):
--      args[0] = ArtMethod*, args[1] = this,
--      args[2] = first Java param, args[3] = second, ...
--------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n[1] TrustManagerImpl...")

-- verifyChain: save untrustedChain arg, return it in onLeave
local _saved_chain = nil
safe_hook(
    "com/android/org/conscrypt/TrustManagerImpl",
    "verifyChain",
    "(Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;[Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;Ljava/lang/String;Ljava/lang/String;Ljava/lang/String;Z)Ljava/util/List;",
    {
        onEnter = function(args)
            _saved_chain = args[2]  -- untrustedChain (first Java param)
            print("  [*] TrustManagerImpl.verifyChain intercepted")
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            if _saved_chain then return _saved_chain end
            return retval.raw
        end
    },
    "TrustManagerImpl.verifyChain (sig1)"
)

-- Alternative signature (Android 7+)
local _saved_chain2 = nil
safe_hook(
    "com/android/org/conscrypt/TrustManagerImpl",
    "verifyChain",
    "([Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;[B[BLjava/lang/String;Z)Ljava/util/List;",
    {
        onEnter = function(args)
            _saved_chain2 = args[2]
            print("  [*] TrustManagerImpl.verifyChain intercepted (sig2)")
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            if _saved_chain2 then return _saved_chain2 end
            return retval.raw
        end
    },
    "TrustManagerImpl.verifyChain (sig2)"
)

-- checkTrustedRecursive
safe_hook(
    "com/android/org/conscrypt/TrustManagerImpl",
    "checkTrustedRecursive",
    "([Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;Ljava/lang/String;Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;Z[B[BLjava/util/ArrayList;)Ljava/util/ArrayList;",
    {
        onEnter = function(args)
            print("  [*] TrustManagerImpl.checkTrustedRecursive intercepted")

            return retval.raw
        end
    },
    "TrustManagerImpl.checkTrustedRecursive"
)

-- 14. Appmattus Certificate Transparency
--------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n[13] Appmattus Certificate Transparency...")

-- 14a. Hook verifyCertificateTransparency on the base class.
-- All Appmattus verifiers (TrustManager, HostnameVerifier, Interceptor) call this.
-- Returning null (0) makes the Kotlin `is VerificationResult.Failure` check false,
-- so the caller proceeds without throwing.
safe_hook(
    "com/appmattus/certificatetransparency/internal/verifier/CertificateTransparencyBase",
    "verifyCertificateTransparency",
    "(Ljava/lang/String;Ljava/util/List;)Lcom/appmattus/certificatetransparency/VerificationResult;",
    {
        onEnter = function(args)
            args.skip = true
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print("  [*] Appmattus CT verification bypassed (base)")
            return 0
        end
    },
    "Appmattus CT Base"
)

-- 14b. HostnameVerifier: direct boolean return as fallback
safe_hook(
    "com/appmattus/certificatetransparency/internal/verifier/CertificateTransparencyHostnameVerifier",

    "(Ljava/lang/String;Ljavax/net/ssl/SSLSession;)Z",
    {
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print("  [*] Appmattus CT HostnameVerifier -> true")
            return 1

)

-- 14c. TrustManager checkServerTrusted (void)

    "checkServerTrusted",
    "([Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;Ljava/lang/String;)V",
    {
        onEnter = function(args)
            args.skip = true
            print("  [*] Appmattus CertificateTransparencyTrustManager bypassed")
        end
    },
    "Appmattus TrustManager (void)"
)

-- 14d. TrustManager checkServerTrusted (List-returning)
local _ct_Arrays = nil
do
    local ok, arr = pcall(Java.use, "java/util/Arrays")
    if ok then _ct_Arrays = arr end
end

local _ct_list = nil
safe_hook(
    "com/appmattus/certificatetransparency/internal/verifier/CertificateTransparencyTrustManager",
    "checkServerTrusted",
    "([Ljava/security/cert/X509Certificate;Ljava/lang/String;Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/util/List;",
    {
        onEnter = function(args)
            if _ct_Arrays then
                local ok, list = pcall(function()
                    return _ct_Arrays:call("asList", "([Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/util/List;", args[2])
                end)
                if ok and list then _ct_list = list end
            end
            args.skip = true
            print("  [*] Appmattus CertificateTransparencyTrustManager (List) bypassed")
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            if _ct_list then return _ct_list.raw end
            return 0
        end
    },
    "Appmattus TrustManager (List)"
)

--------------------------------------------------------------
-- 15. Flutter SSL (native - libflutter.so)
--------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n[14] Flutter SSL bypass (native)...")

local flutter_base = Module.find("libflutter.so")
if flutter_base then
    print(string.format("  libflutter.so @ 0x%x", flutter_base))

    local hooked = false

    -- Phase 1: Try known hardcoded offsets (fast)
    local flutter_ssl_offsets = {
        0x5dc730,  -- Flutter 3.x common
        0x5e5730,
        0x5f1730,
        0x673740,
    }

    for _, offset in ipairs(flutter_ssl_offsets) do
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            hook("libflutter.so", offset, {
                onLeave = function(retval)
                    return 1  -- SSL verify success
                end
            })
        end)
        if ok then
            bypass_count = bypass_count + 1
            print(GREEN .. string.format("  [+] Flutter SSL verify hooked at offset 0x%x", offset) .. RESET)
            hooked = true
            break
        end
    end

    -- Phase 2: Pattern scanning (more robust, covers more versions)
    -- ARM64 patterns for ssl_verify_peer_cert (from NVISO/mixunpin)
    -- Nibble wildcards (F?) converted to full-byte wildcards (??)
    if not hooked then
        print("  Trying pattern scan for ssl_crypto_x509_session_verify_cert_chain...")

        local flutter_patterns = {
            "?? 0F 1C F8 ?? ?? 01 A9 ?? ?? 02 A9 ?? ?? 03 A9 ?? ?? ?? ?? 68 1A 40 F9",
            "?? 43 01 D1 FE 67 01 A9 F8 5F 02 A9 F6 57 03 A9 F4 4F 04 A9 13 00 40 F9 F4 03 00 AA 68 1A 40 F9",
            "FF 43 01 D1 FE 67 01 A9 ?? ?? 06 94 ?? ?? 06 94 68 1A 40 F9 15 15 41 F9 B5 00 00 B4 B6 4A 40 F9",
        }

        for _, pattern in ipairs(flutter_patterns) do
            local ok, results = pcall(function()
                return Memory.scan(pattern, "libflutter.so")
            end)
            if ok and results and #results > 0 then
                local match = results[1]
                local offset = match.offset
                local ok2 = pcall(function()
                    hook("libflutter.so", offset, {
                        onLeave = function(retval)
                            return 1
                        end
                    })
                end)
                if ok2 then
                    bypass_count = bypass_count + 1
                    print(GREEN .. string.format("  [+] Flutter SSL verify found via pattern at offset 0x%x", offset) .. RESET)
                    hooked = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if not hooked then
        print(YELLOW .. "  [-] No Flutter SSL offset matched. Use 'hookgen libflutter.so' to find it." .. RESET)
    end
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  [-] libflutter.so not loaded (not a Flutter app)" .. RESET)

    -- Hook linker to detect late-loaded Flutter
    local linker = Module.find("linker64")
    if linker then
        local symbols = Module.symbols("linker64")
        if symbols then
            for _, sym in ipairs(symbols) do
                if sym.name and sym.name:find("do_dlopen") then
                    local ok = pcall(function()
                        hook("linker64", sym.offset, {
                            onLeave = function()
                                local base = Module.find("libflutter.so")
                                if base then
                                    print(CYAN .. "  [*] libflutter.so loaded! Reload ssl_unpin.lua to hook." .. RESET)
                                end
                            end
                        })
                    end)
                    if ok then
                        print("  [*] Linker hook installed for late-loaded libraries")
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------
-- 15. Native OpenSSL SSL_CTX_set_verify
--------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n[14] Native OpenSSL bypass...")

local ssl_libs = {"libssl.so", "libboringssl.so"}
for _, lib in ipairs(ssl_libs) do
    local base = Module.find(lib)
    if base then
        local exports = Module.exports(lib)
        if exports then
            for _, exp in ipairs(exports) do
                if exp.name == "SSL_CTX_set_verify" then
                    local ok = pcall(function()
                        hook(lib, exp.offset, {
                            onEnter = function(args)
                                -- Native: args[0]=ctx, args[1]=verify_mode, args[2]=callback
                                args[1] = 0  -- SSL_VERIFY_NONE
                                args[2] = 0  -- NULL callback
                                print("  [*] SSL_CTX_set_verify -> VERIFY_NONE")
                            end
                        })
                    end)
                    if ok then
                        bypass_count = bypass_count + 1
                        print(GREEN .. "  [+] " .. lib .. " SSL_CTX_set_verify hooked" .. RESET)
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------
-- 16. SSLContext + HttpsURLConnection (via Java.registerClass)
--------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n[15] SSLContext + HttpsURLConnection (registerClass)...")

local trust_all_verifier = nil
local trust_all_tm = nil
local tm_array = nil
local ok_rc = pcall(function()
    trust_all_verifier = Java.registerClass({
        implements = {"javax/net/ssl/HostnameVerifier"},
        methods = {
            verify = function() return true end
        }
    })

    trust_all_tm = Java.registerClass({
        implements = {"javax/net/ssl/X509TrustManager"},
        methods = {
            checkClientTrusted = function() end,
            checkServerTrusted = function() end,
            getAcceptedIssuers = function() return nil end
        }
    })

    tm_array = Java.array("javax/net/ssl/TrustManager", { trust_all_tm })
end)

if ok_rc and trust_all_verifier and tm_array then
    print(GREEN .. "  [+] Trust-all HostnameVerifier created" .. RESET)
    print(GREEN .. "  [+] Trust-all X509TrustManager created" .. RESET)

    local trust_all_factory = nil
    local ok_factory = pcall(function()
        local SSLCtx = Java.use("javax.net.ssl.SSLContext")
        local ctx = SSLCtx:call("getInstance", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljavax/net/ssl/SSLContext;", "TLS")
        ctx:call("init", "([Ljavax/net/ssl/KeyManager;[Ljavax/net/ssl/TrustManager;Ljava/security/SecureRandom;)V", nil, tm_array, nil)
        trust_all_factory = ctx:call("getSocketFactory", "()Ljavax/net/ssl/SSLSocketFactory;")
    end)

    if ok_factory and trust_all_factory then
        print(GREEN .. "  [+] Trust-all SSLSocketFactory created" .. RESET)
    end

    safe_hook(
        "javax/net/ssl/SSLContext",
        "init",
        "([Ljavax/net/ssl/KeyManager;[Ljavax/net/ssl/TrustManager;Ljava/security/SecureRandom;)V",
        {
            onEnter = function(args)
                args[3] = tm_array.raw
                print("  [*] SSLContext.init -> trust-all TrustManager injected")
            end
        },
        "SSLContext.init"
    )

    safe_hook(
        "javax/net/ssl/HttpsURLConnection",
        "setDefaultHostnameVerifier",
        "(Ljavax/net/ssl/HostnameVerifier;)V",
        {
            onEnter = function(args)
                args[1] = trust_all_verifier.raw
                print("  [*] HttpsURLConnection.setDefaultHostnameVerifier -> trust-all")
            end
        },
        "HttpsURLConnection.setDefaultHostnameVerifier"
    )

    safe_hook(
        "javax/net/ssl/HttpsURLConnection",
        "setHostnameVerifier",
        "(Ljavax/net/ssl/HostnameVerifier;)V",
        {
            onEnter = function(args)
                args[2] = trust_all_verifier.raw
                print("  [*] HttpsURLConnection.setHostnameVerifier -> trust-all")
            end
        },
        "HttpsURLConnection.setHostnameVerifier"
    )

    safe_hook(
        "javax/net/ssl/HttpsURLConnection",
        "getDefaultHostnameVerifier",
        "()Ljavax/net/ssl/HostnameVerifier;",
        {
            onLeave = function(retval)
                print("  [*] HttpsURLConnection.getDefaultHostnameVerifier -> trust-all")
                return trust_all_verifier.raw
            end
        },
        "HttpsURLConnection.getDefaultHostnameVerifier"
    )

    if trust_all_factory then
        safe_hook(
            "javax/net/ssl/HttpsURLConnection",
            "setSSLSocketFactory",
            "(Ljavax/net/ssl/SSLSocketFactory;)V",
            {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    args[2] = trust_all_factory.raw
                    print("  [*] HttpsURLConnection.setSSLSocketFactory -> trust-all")
                end
            },
            "HttpsURLConnection.setSSLSocketFactory"
        )

        safe_hook(
            "javax/net/ssl/HttpsURLConnection",
            "setDefaultSSLSocketFactory",
            "(Ljavax/net/ssl/SSLSocketFactory;)V",
            {
                onEnter = function(args)
                    args[1] = trust_all_factory.raw
                    print("  [*] HttpsURLConnection.setDefaultSSLSocketFactory -> trust-all")
                end
            },
            "HttpsURLConnection.setDefaultSSLSocketFactory"
        )
    end
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  [-] Java.registerClass/array not available" .. RESET)
end

--------------------------------------------------------------
-- Summary
--------------------------------------------------------------
print(string.format("\n" .. CYAN .. "=== %d bypass(es) active ===" .. RESET, bypass_count))
if bypass_count > 0 then
    print(GREEN .. "SSL pinning bypass loaded. Use 'watch' to monitor." .. RESET)
else
    print(RED .. "No bypasses were installed. Check if the app uses custom pinning." .. RESET)
end
```

```lua
-- Strace Example: Custom filtering with Lua callback
-- Only logs openat calls that access specific paths
-- Usage: renef> l scripts/examples/strace_custom.lua -w

print("=== Custom Strace Filter ===")
print("Filtering openat calls for /data/ paths only")

Syscall.trace("openat", {
    onCall = function(info)
        local formatted = info.formatted
        -- Only print if path contains "/data/"
        if formatted and formatted:find("/data/") then
            print(CYAN .. formatted .. RESET)
        end
    end
})

print("")
print("Watching openat for /data/ paths...")
print("Run 'exec Syscall.stop()' to stop.")

```

```lua
-- Strace Example: Network operations tracing with custom callback
-- Usage: renef> l scripts/examples/strace_network.lua -w

print("=== Network Syscall Tracing ===")

-- Trace network category with custom callback to filter output
Syscall.trace("connect", "sendto", "recvfrom", "socket", {
    onCall = function(info)
        -- Only print, formatted output is already available
        print(info.formatted)
    end,
    onReturn = function(info)
        if info.retval < 0 then
            print("  -> ERROR: " .. (info.errno_str or "unknown"))
        end
    end
})
```

`renef_mcp/scripts/syscall_test.lua`:

```lua
-- Syscall Arg Mutation Test
-- Does not require KCOV - runs directly on device
-- Usage: spawn <app> -> l scripts/examples/syscall_test.lua

print(CYAN .. "=== Syscall Arg Mutation Test ===" .. RESET)

local pass = 0
local fail = 0

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 1: onCall arg reading (read-only)
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[1] onCall arg read test...")

local captured_args = nil
Syscall.trace("openat", {
    onCall = function(info)
        if info.args[2] and tostring(info.args[2]) ~= "0" then
            captured_args = {info.args[1], info.args[2], info.args[3]}
            print(string.format("  args: fd=%d, path=0x%x, flags=0x%x",
                  info.args[1], info.args[2], info.args[3]))
        end
    end
})

-- trigger
local f = io.open("/proc/self/maps", "r")
if f then f:close() end

Syscall.untrace("openat")

if captured_args then
    print(GREEN .. "  PASS: onCall args read" .. RESET)
    pass = pass + 1
else
    print(RED .. "  FAIL: onCall not triggered" .. RESET)
    fail = fail + 1
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 2: onReturn retval reading
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[2] onReturn retval test...")

local ret_captured = false
Syscall.trace("openat", {
    onReturn = function(info)
        if info.retval and info.retval >= 0 then
            print(string.format("  retval: %d (fd)", info.retval))
            ret_captured = true
        end
    end
})

local f2 = io.open("/proc/self/status", "r")
if f2 then f2:close() end

Syscall.untrace("openat")

if ret_captured then
    print(GREEN .. "  PASS: onReturn retval read" .. RESET)
    pass = pass + 1
else
    print(RED .. "  FAIL: onReturn not triggered" .. RESET)
    fail = fail + 1
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 3: onReturn retval override
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[3] onReturn retval override test...")

local original_ret = nil
local override_worked = false

Syscall.trace("access", {
    onCall = function(info)
        print("  access() called")
    end,
    onReturn = function(info)
        original_ret = info.retval
        print(string.format("  original retval: %d", info.retval))
        -- return 0 to always report "success"
        return 0
    end
})

-- nonexistent file - normally returns -1
os.execute("test -f /nonexistent_file_xyztmp 2>/dev/null")

Syscall.untrace("access")

-- Note: full verification via os.execute is difficult,
-- but seeing the callback fire and return is sufficient
if original_ret ~= nil then
    print(GREEN .. "  PASS: retval override callback fired" .. RESET)
    pass = pass + 1
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  SKIP: access() not triggered" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 4: skip original
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[4] skip original test...")

local skip_tested = false
Syscall.trace("access", {
    onCall = function(info)
        print("  access() caught, skip=true, retval=-1")
        info.skip = true
        info.retval = -1  -- behave like ENOENT
        skip_tested = true
    end,
    onReturn = function(info)
        print(string.format("  retval after skip: %d", info.retval))
        return info.retval
    end
})

-- this syscall should never reach the kernel
os.execute("test -f /proc/self/maps 2>/dev/null")

Syscall.untrace("access")

if skip_tested then
    print(GREEN .. "  PASS: skip original worked" .. RESET)
    pass = pass + 1
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  SKIP: access() not triggered" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 5: arg mutation (ioctl)
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[5] ioctl arg mutation test...")

local ioctl_seen = false
local mutated = false

Syscall.trace("ioctl", {
    onCall = function(info)
        if not ioctl_seen then
            ioctl_seen = true
            local orig_cmd = info.args[2]
            -- modify cmd (just to verify mutation works)
            -- in real fuzzing you'd assign a random value here
            info.args[2] = orig_cmd
            mutated = true
            print(string.format("  ioctl fd=%d cmd=0x%x -> mutated", info.args[1], orig_cmd))
        end
    end
})

-- wait briefly, ioctl will come naturally (binder etc.)
-- or trigger one
local _ = os.clock()

Syscall.untrace("ioctl")

if mutated then
    print(GREEN .. "  PASS: ioctl arg mutation worked" .. RESET)
    pass = pass + 1
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  SKIP: ioctl not triggered (may be normal)" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Summary
-----------------------------------------------
Syscall.stop()

print(CYAN .. string.format("\n=== Result: %d PASS, %d FAIL ===", pass, fail) .. RESET)

```

`renef_mcp/scripts/thread_test.lua`:

```lua
-- Thread backtrace test script

local tid = Thread.id()
print(string.format("pid: %d, tid: %d", tid, tid))
print("backtrace:")

local bt = Thread.backtrace()

for i, frame in ipairs(bt) do
    local pc_str = string.format("%016x", frame.pc)
    local offset_str = ""
    local symbol_str = ""
    local path_str = frame.path or frame.module or "<unknown>"

    if frame.offset then
        offset_str = string.format("+%d", frame.offset)
    end

    if frame.symbol then
        symbol_str = string.format(" (%s%s)", frame.symbol, offset_str)
    elseif frame.offset then
        symbol_str = string.format(" (offset 0x%x)", frame.offset)
    end

    print(string.format("    #%02d pc %s  %s%s",
        frame.index,
        pc_str,
        path_str,
        symbol_str))
end

```

`renef_mcp/server.py`:

```py
"""android-renef-mcp: MCP server exposing Renef to AI agents (stdio transport).

Architecture notes (important for maintenance):
- renef_server on the device is a SINGLE-CLIENT TCP server. renef-mcp keeps one
  TCP connection for everything (status commands and the instrumented session)
  and at most one attached target, all serialized by a lock.
- `exec` responses: the server relay only exits early on a "✓ Lua executed"/
  "✗ Lua" marker, otherwise it lingers ~5s. renef_mcp.client.wrap_lua() makes
  every exec print the marker itself, so execs return promptly and reliably.
- Module helpers are implemented over exec with correct Lua (the upstream C
  binding's Module.list wrapper is broken: it ipairs-iterates a string).
"""

import os
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path

from mcp.server.fastmcp import FastMCP

from renef_mcp import adb, config
from renef_mcp import scripts as renef_scripts
from renef_mcp import techniques as technique_docs
from renef_mcp.client import RenefError
from renef_mcp.lua import normalize_hex_pattern, quote
from renef_mcp.session import MANAGER, NoSessionError

mcp = FastMCP(
    "renef",
    instructions=(
        "Renef MCP: dynamic instrumentation for Android ARM64 (Frida-like, Lua scripting).\n"
        "Workflow: renef_status -> renef_attach(pid) or renef_spawn(package) -> "
        "renef_exec(lua) / hook tools. Read resource renef://docs/lua-api for the full "
        "Lua API (Module/Memory/hook/Java/Syscall/Thread/File/OS/KCov). Renef is NOT "
        "Frida: no Interceptor/Java.perform. Java class names use slashes, e.g. "
        "'com/example/Class'. Use pcall() around hooks for classes that may not exist."
    ),
)

MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS = 60_000
MAX_MEM_READ = 4096

# On-device install paths (match the upstream server's RENEF_PAYLOAD_PATH).
SERVER_BIN = "/data/local/tmp/renef_server"
SERVER_LOG = "/data/local/tmp/renef_server.log"
AGENT_BIN = "/data/local/tmp/libagent.so"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _truncate(text: str, limit: int = MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS) -> str:
    if len(text) <= limit:
        return text
    return text[:limit] + f"\n... [truncated, {len(text) - limit} chars dropped]"


def _run_lua(lua_code: str, total_cap: float = 30.0) -> str:
    """exec Lua in the target; return cleaned output (raises RenefError on Lua error)."""
    is_err, text = MANAGER.exec(lua_code, total_cap=total_cap)
    if is_err:
        raise RenefError(f"Lua execution failed:\n{text}")
    return text or "(no output)"


def _require_session() -> None:
    if not MANAGER.is_active():
        raise NoSessionError(
            "No active target. Use renef_attach(pid) or renef_spawn(package) first "
            "(renef_list_processes / renef_list_apps help find targets)."
        )


def _hexdump(data: bytes, base: int) -> str:
    lines = []
    for off in range(0, len(data), 16):
        row = data[off : off + 16]
        hex_part = " ".join(f"{b:02x}" for b in row).ljust(16 * 3 - 1)
        ascii_part = "".join(chr(b) if 32 <= b < 127 else "." for b in row)
        lines.append(f"0x{base + off:016x}  {hex_part}  |{ascii_part}|")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _to_int(value: str, what: str) -> int:
    try:
        result = int(value, 0)  # accepts 0x.. and decimal
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as e:
        raise ValueError(f"{what} must be an integer (dec or 0x-prefixed), got {value!r}") from e
    if result < 0:
        raise ValueError(f"{what} must be non-negative, got {value!r}")
    return result


def _format_symbols(items: list, filter_sub: str, limit: int) -> str:
    if filter_sub:
        f = filter_sub.lower()
        items = [i for i in items if f in i["name"].lower()]
    total = len(items)
    shown = items[: max(1, int(limit))]
    body = "\n".join(f"0x{i['offset']:x}  {i['name']}" for i in shown)
    header = f"{total} match(es)" + (f" (showing {len(shown)})" if total > len(shown) else "")
    return _truncate(f"{header}:\n{body}" if body else f"{header}")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# status / discovery tools
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _status_text() -> str:
    lines = []

    try:
        serials = adb.device_serials()
        lines.append(f"adb devices: {', '.join(serials) if serials else '(none)'}")
    except adb.AdbError as e:
        lines.append(f"adb devices: ERROR ({e})")
        serials = []

    if config.uses_local_forward():
        try:
            lines.append("adb forward: " + adb.ensure_forward())
        except adb.AdbError as e:
            lines.append(f"adb forward: ERROR ({e})")
    else:
        lines.append(f"adb forward: n/a (direct TCP to {config.host()})")

    # Probe over a separate short-lived socket: a status check must never
    # touch (let alone close) the client that owns the live target session.
    try:
        reachable = adb.ping_server()
        lines.append(f"renef_server ping: {'pong (reachable)' if reachable else 'NO RESPONSE'}")
        if not reachable:
            lines.append(
                '  hint: start it with  adb shell "su -c \'/data/local/tmp/renef_server\'"'
            )
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        lines.append(f"renef_server ping: ERROR ({e})")

    if MANAGER.is_active():
        try:
            out = _run_lua("print('alive', OS.getpid())")
            lines.append(
                f"session: ACTIVE pid={MANAGER.pid} ({MANAGER.last_action}) eval-check: {out}"
            )
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            lines.append(f"session: ACTIVE pid={MANAGER.pid} but eval failed: {e}")
    else:
        lines.append("session: none (use renef_attach or renef_spawn)")

    return "\n".join(lines)


@mcp.tool()
def renef_status() -> str:
    """Check the whole chain: adb device, adb port forward, renef_server reachability
    (ping/pong), and active target session. Start here."""
    return _status_text()


@mcp.tool()
def renef_list_apps(third_party_only: bool = False) -> str:
    """List installed packages on the device (via adb, no session needed).
    Set third_party_only=true to skip system packages."""
    pkgs = adb.list_packages(third_party_only=third_party_only)
    if not pkgs:
        return "(no packages matched)"
    return _truncate(f"{len(pkgs)} packages:\n" + "\n".join(pkgs))


@mcp.tool()
def renef_list_processes() -> str:
    """List running userland processes (pid + name) on the device, for renef_attach."""
    procs = adb.list_processes()
    if not procs:
        return "(no processes found)"
    return _truncate(
        f"{len(procs)} processes:\n"
        + "\n".join(f"{p['pid']:>7}  {p['name']}" for p in procs)
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# server lifecycle tools (adb only, no session needed)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _parse_md5sums(text: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Parse `md5sum` output, tolerating the binary-mode `*path` form."""
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in text.splitlines():
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) == 2:
            # binary mode is "hash *path" (star BEFORE the path); be lenient
            # and also tolerate a trailing star from other implementations.
            out[parts[1].lstrip("*").rstrip("*")] = parts[0]
    return out


def _extract_archive_legacy(tf, dest: Path) -> None:
    """Safe extraction for Python 3.10.0-3.10.11 / 3.11.0-3.11.3 (no PEP 706)."""
    root = dest.resolve()
    for member in tf.getmembers():
        target = (root / member.name).resolve()
        if (not str(target).startswith(str(root) + os.sep)
                or member.issym() or member.islnk() or member.isdev()):
            raise RenefError(f"unsafe archive member: {member.name}")
    tf.extractall(dest)


def _package_version() -> str:
    """This package's version (used to locate the matching binaries release)."""
    from renef_mcp import __version__

    return __version__


def _download_binaries() -> Path:
    """Fetch the pinned, CI-built renef binaries for this package version.

    Trust chain: the binaries are built by this repo's GitHub Actions from the
    renef source pinned in ``renef_mcp/BINARIES_PIN``, attached to the release
    matching this package version, and verified here against the SHA256SUMS.txt
    published alongside them.
    """
    import hashlib
    import shutil
    import tarfile
    import urllib.request

    tag = "v" + _package_version()
    cache = Path.home() / ".cache" / "android-renef-mcp" / tag
    server_bin, agent_bin = cache / "renef_server", cache / "libagent.so"
    if server_bin.is_file() and agent_bin.is_file():
        return cache

    def _fetch(url: str, dest: Path) -> None:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=180) as resp, open(dest, "wb") as f:
            shutil.copyfileobj(resp, f)

    def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
        return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

    def _expected_hash(sums: Path) -> str | None:
        hashes = _parse_md5sums(sums.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return next((v for k, v in hashes.items()
                     if k.endswith("renef-android-arm64.tar.gz")), None)

    cache.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    base = f"https://github.com/FlyPhantomFly/android-renef-mcp/releases/download/{tag}"
    tarball, sums = cache / "renef-android-arm64.tar.gz", cache / "SHA256SUMS.txt"

    # Cached files are re-verified on every use (a corrupted extraction must
    # never be deployed). Only the small checksum file is fetched if it is
    # missing; the tarball is re-downloaded if anything is stale.
    if not (server_bin.is_file() and agent_bin.is_file()
            and tarball.is_file() and sums.is_file()
            and _sha256(tarball) == _expected_hash(sums)):
        _fetch(f"{base}/renef-android-arm64.tar.gz", tarball)
        _fetch(f"{base}/SHA256SUMS.txt", sums)

    expected = _expected_hash(sums)
    if not expected or _sha256(tarball) != expected:
        shutil.rmtree(cache, ignore_errors=True)
        raise RenefError(
            "downloaded renef-android-arm64.tar.gz failed SHA256 verification "
            f"(sha256 {_sha256(tarball)}, expected {expected or 'none'}); refusing to deploy"
        )

    def _extract(tf) -> None:
        try:
            tf.extractall(cache, filter="data")  # PEP 706, Python >= 3.12 / backports
        except TypeError:
            _extract_archive_legacy(tf, cache)

    with tarfile.open(tarball) as tf:
        _extract(tf)
    if not (server_bin.is_file() and agent_bin.is_file()):
        shutil.rmtree(cache, ignore_errors=True)
        raise RenefError("downloaded archive did not contain renef_server and libagent.so")
    return cache


def _resolve_bin_dir(source: str) -> tuple[Path, str]:
    """Where the deployable binaries live, per ``source`` mode. Returns (dir, how)."""
    if source == "download":
        # An explicit download request must not be silently overridden by
        # RENEF_BIN_DIR (documented as "always download").
        return _download_binaries(), "downloaded pinned CI build"
    env = os.environ.get("RENEF_BIN_DIR", "").strip()
    if env:
        return Path(env).expanduser().resolve(), "RENEF_BIN_DIR"
    local = config.renef_home() / "build" / "android"
    if (local / "renef_server").is_file() and source in ("auto", "build"):
        return local, "local build"
    if source == "auto":
        return _download_binaries(), "downloaded pinned CI build"
    raise FileNotFoundError(
        f"no local renef build at {local} and source='build' requires one "
        f"(cd {config.renef_home()} && make, or set RENEF_BIN_DIR)"
    )


def _server_pid() -> str:
    try:
        return adb.run_adb("shell", "pidof", "renef_server").strip()
    except adb.AdbError:
        return ""


@mcp.tool()
def renef_server_deploy(source: str = "auto") -> str:
    """Push the built renef_server + libagent.so to the device, set permissions
    and the SELinux context the agent needs, and verify by md5.
    source="auto" (default): prefer a local build (RENEF_BIN_DIR, else
    <RENEF_HOME>/build/android); if none exists, download the pinned CI-built
    binaries from this package's GitHub release and SHA256-verify them.
    source="build": require the local build. source="download": always download."""
    source = str(source).strip().lower()
    if source not in ("auto", "build", "download"):
        raise ValueError("source must be 'auto', 'build' or 'download'")
    import hashlib

    bdir, how = _resolve_bin_dir(source)
    server = bdir / "renef_server"
    agent = bdir / "libagent.so"
    for path in (server, agent):
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                f"{path} not found (source={source}, resolved via {how}). "
                "Build Renef first (cd <RENEF_HOME> && make) or use source='download'."
            )
    adb.run_adb("push", str(server), SERVER_BIN + ".new", timeout=120)
    adb.run_adb("push", str(agent), AGENT_BIN + ".new", timeout=120)
    try:
        adb.run_adb(
            "shell",
            f"su -c 'mv -f {SERVER_BIN}.new {SERVER_BIN} && "
            f"mv -f {AGENT_BIN}.new {AGENT_BIN} && "
            f"chmod 755 {SERVER_BIN} && chmod 777 {AGENT_BIN} && "
            f"chcon u:object_r:app_data_file:s0 {AGENT_BIN} && "
            f"chcon u:object_r:shell_data_file:s0 {SERVER_BIN}'",
            timeout=60,
        )
    finally:
        try:
            adb.run_adb("shell", f"rm -f {SERVER_BIN}.new {AGENT_BIN}.new", timeout=15)
        except adb.AdbError:
            pass

    def _local_md5(path: Path) -> str:
        h = hashlib.md5()
        with path.open("rb") as f:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
                h.update(chunk)
        return h.hexdigest()

    remote = adb.run_adb("shell", f"su -c 'md5sum {SERVER_BIN} {AGENT_BIN}'", timeout=60)
    hashes = _parse_md5sums(remote)
    results = []
    for local, remote_path in ((server, SERVER_BIN), (agent, AGENT_BIN)):
        got = hashes.get(remote_path, "")
        if got == _local_md5(local):
            results.append(f"{remote_path}: md5 verified")
        else:
            results.append(f"{remote_path}: MD5 MISMATCH (got {got or 'none'})")
    results.append(f"agent path {AGENT_BIN} matches this build's RENEF_PAYLOAD_PATH")
    results.append(f"binaries: {how} ({bdir})")
    try:
        ctx = adb.run_adb("shell", f"su -c 'ls -Z {AGENT_BIN}'", timeout=15).split()[0]
        if "app_data_file" not in ctx:
            results.append(f"WARNING: agent context is {ctx}; targets may not be able to map it")
        else:
            results.append(f"agent context: {ctx}")
    except adb.AdbError:
        pass
    if not all("verified" in r for r in results[:2]):
        raise RenefError("deploy verification failed:\n" + "\n".join(results))
    return "\n".join(results)


@mcp.tool()
def renef_server_start() -> str:
    """Start renef_server on the device as root (idempotent) and set up the adb
    port forward. Verifies with a ping."""
    pid = _server_pid()
    if pid:
        reachable = adb.ping_server()
        return (f"renef_server already running (pid {pid}, "
                f"{'reachable' if reachable else 'NO RESPONSE to ping'})")
    adb.ensure_forward()
    # One shell string: adb joins argv with spaces, so `su -c` must receive a
    # single quoted payload or it only takes the first word as the command.
    adb.run_adb("shell", f"su -c 'nohup {SERVER_BIN} > {SERVER_LOG} 2>&1 &'", timeout=30)
    time.sleep(0.8)
    pid = _server_pid()
    if not pid:
        raise RenefError(
            f"renef_server did not start (no pid). Check the log: adb shell \"su -c 'tail {SERVER_LOG}'\" "
            "or renef_server_logs. Is the device rooted?"
        )
    reachable = adb.ping_server()
    status = "reachable" if reachable else "started but ping failed (may still be binding)"
    return f"renef_server started (pid {pid}, {status}); adb forward set"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_server_stop() -> str:
    """Stop renef_server on the device (kill as root)."""
    pid = _server_pid()
    if not pid:
        return "renef_server is not running"
    adb.run_adb("shell", f"su -c 'kill -9 {pid}'", timeout=30)
    time.sleep(0.3)
    return f"renef_server (pid {pid}) stopped" if not _server_pid() \
        else f"kill sent to pid {pid} but it is still running"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_server_restart() -> str:
    """Restart renef_server on the device (stop, then start with port forward)."""
    stopped = renef_server_stop()
    if "not running" not in stopped and "still running" not in stopped:
        time.sleep(0.5)
    return stopped + "\n" + renef_server_start()


@mcp.tool()
def renef_server_logs(lines: int = 60) -> str:
    """Tail the renef_server log from the device (last <lines> lines, default 60)."""
    lines = max(1, min(int(lines), 500))
    try:
        out = adb.run_adb("shell", "tail", "-n", str(lines), SERVER_LOG, timeout=30)
    except adb.AdbError:
        try:
            out = adb.run_adb("shell", "tail", "-n", str(lines),
                              "/data/local/tmp/renef.log", timeout=30)
        except adb.AdbError as e:
            raise RenefError(
                f"could not read the server log ({e}). If the server was started "
                "manually without redirection, there is no log file."
            ) from e
    return _truncate(out.strip() or "(log file is empty)")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# session tools
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@mcp.tool()
def renef_attach(pid: int) -> str:
    """Attach to a running process and inject the renef agent."""
    try:
        new_pid = MANAGER.attach(int(pid))
    except (RenefError, OSError):
        MANAGER.disconnect()
        raise
    # verify the agent responds so failures surface immediately
    try:
        _run_lua(f"print('attached ok, pid={new_pid}')")
    except Exception:
        MANAGER.disconnect()
        raise RenefError(
            f"attach({pid}) was accepted but agent eval failed - injection may have failed"
        )
    return f"attached to pid {new_pid}. Use renef_exec to run Lua in it."


@mcp.tool()
def renef_spawn(package: str) -> str:
    """Spawn an app by package name, gated at startup (ptrace pause), inject the agent.
    The first renef_exec auto-resumes the gated process; renef_resume resumes it
    without running a script."""
    package = package.strip()
    if not package:
        raise ValueError("package must not be empty")
    try:
        new_pid = MANAGER.spawn(package)
    except (RenefError, OSError):
        MANAGER.disconnect()
        raise
    return (
        f"spawned {package} (pid {new_pid}, gated at startup). "
        "Run renef_exec to deliver a script; it auto-resumes the process. "
        "Use renef_resume to resume without running a script."
    )


@mcp.tool()
def renef_resume() -> str:
    """Resume a spawn-gated process (after renef_spawn)."""
    def _resume(client) -> str:
        return client.resume()

    out = str(MANAGER.call(_resume))
    return out or "(no response from server)"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_disconnect() -> str:
    """Close the current target session (does not kill the target process)."""
    was = MANAGER.is_active()
    MANAGER.disconnect()
    return "session closed" if was else "no session was open"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# execution tools
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@mcp.tool()
def renef_exec(lua: str) -> str:
    """Execute Lua code in the target process and return print() output.
    Main tool. Examples: "print(Module.list())", "print(Memory.readString(args[0]))".
    Multi-line Lua is fine. See resource renef://docs/lua-api for the full API."""
    _require_session()
    return _truncate(_run_lua(lua))


@mcp.tool()
def renef_load_script(path: str) -> str:
    """Load a Lua script file from the MCP host machine and run it in the target."""
    _require_session()

    def _load(client):
        return client.load_script(path)

    is_err, text = MANAGER.call(_load)
    if is_err:
        raise RenefError(f"script failed:\n{text}")
    return _truncate(text or "(script ran, no output)")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# memory tools
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_scan(pattern: str, module: str = "") -> str:
    """Scan memory for a hex pattern. Prefer module="libfoo.so": it uses Lua
    Memory.search and supports spaces and ?? wildcards (e.g. "CA FE ?? BE").
    Without module, the upstream full-process scan is used: compact hex only and
    it can exceed the server's ~10s response cap (an error will say so)."""
    _require_session()
    # Validate locally: the upstream ms command silently corrupts spaced or
    # wildcard patterns instead of rejecting them.
    normalize_hex_pattern(pattern, wildcards=bool(module.strip()))

    def _scan(client) -> str:
        return client.memscan(pattern, module)

    raw = str(MANAGER.call(_scan))
    return _truncate(raw.strip() or "(no matches)")


@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_read(addr: str, size: int = 64) -> str:
    """Read bytes from target memory and return a hexdump. addr accepts decimal or 0x-hex.
    size is capped at 4096 bytes."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(addr, "addr")
    size = max(1, min(int(size), MAX_MEM_READ))
    return _mem_read_impl(address, size)


def _mem_read_impl(address: int, size: int) -> str:
    is_err, text = MANAGER.exec(
        "local data = Memory.read("
        f"0x{address:x}, {size}); "
        "if not data then print('__MEMFAIL__') else "
        "local hex = ''; for i = 1, #data do hex = hex .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(data, i)) end; "
        "print(hex) end",
        total_cap=15.0,
    )
    if is_err:
        raise RenefError(f"Memory.read failed: {text}")
    hex_text = text.strip().splitlines()[-1] if text.strip() else ""
    if not hex_text or "__MEMFAIL__" in hex_text:
        raise RenefError(f"could not read {size} bytes at 0x{address:x} (unmapped or protected?)")
    hex_text = "".join(hex_text.split())
    try:
        data = bytes.fromhex(hex_text)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise RenefError(f"bad hex response from agent: {hex_text[:64]!r}") from e
    return _hexdump(data[:size], address)


@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_read_value(addr: str, size: int = 4, signed: bool = False) -> str:
    """Read an integer from target memory. size: 1, 2, 4 or 8 bytes (unsigned unless signed=true)."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(addr, "addr")
    size = int(size)
    if size not in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        raise ValueError("size must be 1, 2, 4 or 8")
    lua = f"local v = Memory.readU{size * 8}(0x{address:x}); print(v or '__NIL__')"
    out = _run_lua(lua, total_cap=10.0)
    val_s = out.strip().splitlines()[-1] if out.strip() else "__NIL__"
    if "__NIL__" in val_s:
        raise RenefError(f"could not read {size}B at 0x{address:x}")
    try:
        value = int(val_s)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise RenefError(f"unexpected agent response: {val_s!r}") from e
    if signed and value >= (1 << (size * 8 - 1)):
        value -= 1 << (size * 8)
    mask = (1 << (size * 8)) - 1
    return f"{addr} ({size}B) = {value} (0x{value & mask:x})"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_read_string(addr: str, max_len: int = 256) -> str:
    """Read a NUL-terminated C string from target memory."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(addr, "addr")
    max_len = max(1, min(int(max_len), 65536))
    out = _run_lua(
        f"local s = Memory.readString(0x{address:x}, {max_len}); if s then print(s) else print('__NIL__') end",
        total_cap=10.0,
    )
    if "__NIL__" in out:
        raise RenefError(f"could not read string at 0x{address:x}")
    return out


@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_write(addr: str, hex_bytes: str) -> str:
    """Write raw bytes to target memory. hex_bytes: hex string, spaces allowed, e.g. "1F 20 03 D5"."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(addr, "addr")
    clean = "".join(hex_bytes.split())
    if clean.startswith(("0x", "0X")):
        clean = clean[2:]  # only a single leading prefix; bytes.fromhex validates the rest
    if not clean or len(clean) % 2 != 0:
        raise ValueError("hex_bytes must be a non-empty even-length hex string")
    try:
        data = bytes.fromhex(clean)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise ValueError(f"invalid hex: {e}") from e
    lua_bytes = "".join(f"\\x{b:02x}" for b in data)
    is_err, text = MANAGER.exec(
        f"local ok = Memory.write(0x{address:x}, \"{lua_bytes}\"); print(ok and 'WROTE' or '__WFAIL__')",
        total_cap=10.0,
    )
    if is_err or "__WFAIL__" in text:
        raise RenefError(f"write failed at 0x{address:x}: {text}")
    return f"wrote {len(data)} bytes at {addr}"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_write_value(addr: str, value: int, size: int = 4) -> str:
    """Write an integer to target memory. size: 1, 2, 4 or 8 bytes."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(addr, "addr")
    value = int(value)
    size = int(size)
    if size not in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        raise ValueError("size must be 1, 2, 4 or 8")
    if not (0 <= value < (1 << (size * 8))):
        raise ValueError(f"value does not fit in {size} byte(s) as unsigned")
    # Memory.writeU* returns nothing on success (raw dereference) - verify by reading back
    lua = (
        f"Memory.writeU{size * 8}(0x{address:x}, {value}); "
        f"print('VERIFY ' .. string.format('0x%x', Memory.readU{size * 8}(0x{address:x})))"
    )
    is_err, text = MANAGER.exec(lua, total_cap=10.0)
    expected = value & ((1 << (size * 8)) - 1)
    if is_err or f"0x{expected:x}" not in text:
        raise RenefError(
            f"write_value failed at 0x{address:x} (read back {text.strip()!r}, "
            f"expected 0x{expected:x}) - target may have crashed or memory is not writable"
        )
    return f"wrote {value} (0x{value:x}) as {size}B at {addr} (verified)"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_mem_patch(addr: str, hex_bytes: str) -> str:
    """Write raw bytes to target memory using Memory.patch, which handles
    mprotect internally (works on read-only code pages that renef_mem_write
    cannot touch). hex_bytes: hex string, spaces allowed."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(addr, "addr")
    clean = "".join(hex_bytes.split())
    if clean.startswith(("0x", "0X")):
        clean = clean[2:]  # only a single leading prefix; bytes.fromhex validates the rest
    if not clean or len(clean) % 2 != 0:
        raise ValueError("hex_bytes must be a non-empty even-length hex string")
    try:
        data = bytes.fromhex(clean)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise ValueError(f"invalid hex: {e}") from e
    lua_bytes = "".join(f"\\x{b:02x}" for b in data)
    is_err, text = MANAGER.exec(
        f"local ok = Memory.patch(0x{address:x}, \"{lua_bytes}\"); print(ok and 'PATCHED' or '__PFAIL__')",
        total_cap=10.0,
    )
    if is_err or "__PFAIL__" in text:
        raise RenefError(f"patch failed at 0x{address:x}: {text}")
    return f"patched {len(data)} bytes at {addr}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# module tools
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@mcp.tool()
def renef_module_list() -> str:
    """List loaded modules/libraries in the target process (base address, name, path)."""
    _require_session()

    def _list(client):
        return client.module_list()

    mods = MANAGER.call(_list)
    if not mods:
        return "(no modules)"
    return _truncate(
        f"{len(mods)} modules:\n"
        + "\n".join(f"0x{m['base']:016x}  {m['name']}" for m in mods)
    )


@mcp.tool()
def renef_module_find(name: str) -> str:
    """Get the base address of a loaded module, e.g. renef_module_find("libc.so")."""
    _require_session()

    def _find(client):
        return client.module_find(name.strip())

    base = MANAGER.call(_find)
    if base is None:
        return f"module {name!r} not found (check name with renef_module_list)"
    return f"{name} base = 0x{base:x}"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_module_exports(name: str, filter: str = "", limit: int = 200) -> str:
    """List exported symbols of a module. Use filter to substring-match names, limit to cap output."""
    _require_session()

    def _exports(client):
        return client.module_exports(name.strip())

    items = MANAGER.call(_exports)
    if not items:
        return f"no exports for {name!r}"
    return _format_symbols(items, filter, int(limit))


@mcp.tool()
def renef_module_symbols(name: str, filter: str = "", limit: int = 200) -> str:
    """List all symbols (including non-exported) of a module. filter/limit as in renef_module_exports."""
    _require_session()

    def _symbols(client):
        return client.module_symbols(name.strip())

    items = MANAGER.call(_symbols)
    if not items:
        return f"no symbols for {name!r}"
    return _format_symbols(items, filter, int(limit))


@mcp.tool()
def renef_sections(lib: str) -> str:
    """List ELF sections of a loaded library (upstream `sec` command)."""
    _require_session()
    return _truncate(str(MANAGER.call(lambda c: c.sections(lib))))


@mcp.tool()
def renef_hook_generate(lib: str, symbol_or_offset: str = "") -> str:
    """Generate a ready-to-run Lua hook template (upstream `hookgen` command).
    With a symbol it resolves the offset first (needs a session):
    renef_hook_generate("libc.so", "fopen") or renef_hook_generate("libc.so", "0x1a2b3")."""
    _require_session()
    return _truncate(str(MANAGER.call(lambda c: c.hookgen(lib, symbol_or_offset))))


@mcp.tool()
def renef_framework_detect() -> str:
    """Detect the app framework (Flutter, React Native, Unity, Xamarin,
    Cordova/Ionic) by checking for its characteristic libraries. Needs a session."""
    _require_session()
    lua = """
local frameworks = {
    flutter = {"libflutter.so", "libapp.so"},
    react_native = {"libhermes.so", "libreactnativejni.so", "libreactnative.so"},
    unity = {"libunity.so", "libil2cpp.so"},
    xamarin = {"libmono-android.so", "libmono.so", "libmonodroid.so"},
    cordova = {"libcordova.so"},
}
local detected = {}
for framework, libs in pairs(frameworks) do
    for _, lib in ipairs(libs) do
        local base = Module.find(lib)
        if base then
            detected[framework] = detected[framework] or {}
            table.insert(detected[framework], string.format("%s @ 0x%x", lib, base))
        end
    end
end
if next(detected) == nil then
    print("Framework: native (no framework libraries found)")
else
    for framework, libs in pairs(detected) do
        print("Framework: " .. framework)
        for _, line in ipairs(libs) do print("  - " .. line) end
    end
end
"""
    return _truncate(_run_lua(lua, total_cap=20.0))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# hook tools
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@mcp.tool()
def renef_hook_native(lib: str, offset: str, on_enter: str = "", on_leave: str = "",
                      caller: str = "") -> str:
    """Install a native hook at offset (from library base) in lib, e.g. renef_hook_native("libc.so", "0x1234",
    on_enter="print('fopen path:', Memory.readString(args[0]))").
    Callbacks are Lua statement strings using args[0..7] (onEnter) / retval (onLeave).
    Find offsets with renef_module_exports first.
    caller selects the upstream PLT/GOT backend (e.g. caller="libtarget.so");
    omit it for a trampoline hook. There is no separate hook_type: upstream
    ignores the old --hook= argument and auto-selects from caller.
    WARNING: do not hook ultra-hot functions like clock_gettime/read/write - the agent's
    own output path may call them, causing recursive hooks that crash the target."""
    _require_session()
    address = _to_int(offset, "offset")
    callbacks = (
        "{ "
        + (f"onEnter = function(args) {on_enter} end" if on_enter.strip() else "")
        + (", " if on_enter.strip() and on_leave.strip() else "")
        + (f"onLeave = function(retval) {on_leave} end" if on_leave.strip() else "")
        + (", " if caller.strip() and (on_enter.strip() or on_leave.strip()) else "")
        + (f"caller = {quote(caller.strip())}" if caller.strip() else "")
        + " }"
    )
    lua = f"hook({quote(lib.strip())}, 0x{address:x}, {callbacks})"
    _run_lua(lua, total_cap=15.0)
    suffix = f" (caller={caller.strip()})" if caller.strip() else ""
    return f"native hook installed at {lib}+{offset}{suffix}"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_hook_java(class_name: str, method: str, signature: str, on_enter: str = "", on_leave: str = "") -> str:
    """Install a Java method hook. class_name in JNI slash format (e.g. "com/example/Class"),
    signature in JNI format (e.g. "(Ljava/lang/String;)Z").
    arg layout: args[0]=ArtMethod* (ignore), args[1]=this (instance methods), args[2..]=parameters.
    Use on_enter="args.skip = true" to bypass void methods. Wrap uncertain classes in pcall via renef_exec."""
    _require_session()
    lua = (
        f"hook({quote(class_name.strip())}, {quote(method.strip())}, {quote(signature.strip())}, "
        "{ "
        + (f"onEnter = function(args) {on_enter} end" if on_enter.strip() else "")
        + (", " if on_enter.strip() and on_leave.strip() else "")
        + (f"onLeave = function(retval) {on_leave} end" if on_leave.strip() else "")
        + " })"
    )
    _run_lua(lua, total_cap=15.0)
    return f"java hook installed: {class_name}.{method} {signature}"


@mcp.tool()
def renef_list_hooks() -> str:
    """List active native hooks in the target process. Upstream's registry is
    native-only: Java hooks (renef_hook_java) do not appear here and cannot be
    removed by renef_unhook; they are restored when the agent connection closes,
    i.e. on the next attach/spawn."""
    _require_session()

    def _hooks(client) -> str:
        return client.hooks()

    out = str(MANAGER.call(_hooks))
    return _truncate(out or "(no active native hooks)")


@mcp.tool()
def renef_unhook(hook_id: str) -> str:
    """Remove a hook by id (from renef_list_hooks), or "all" to remove every hook."""
    _require_session()
    s = str(hook_id).strip()

    def _unhook(client, hid) -> str:
        return client.unhook(hid)

    if s.lower() == "all":
        listing = str(MANAGER.call(lambda c: c.hooks()))
        ids = sorted(
            int(m.group(1))
            for m in re.finditer(r"(?m)^\s*\[(\d+)\](.*)$", listing or "")
            if "(removed)" not in m.group(2)
        )
        if ids:
            results = []
            for hid in ids:
                results.append(f"#{hid}: {str(MANAGER.call(lambda c, h=hid: c.unhook(h))).strip() or 'ok'}")
            return f"removed {len(ids)} hook(s):\n" + "\n".join(results)
        # Listing unavailable (e.g. a hook print storm hides it): upstream
        # understands a literal `unhook all`, so use it directly.
        raw = str(MANAGER.call(lambda c: c.unhook("all"))).strip()
        if raw.lstrip().lower().startswith(("error", "fail")):
            raise RenefError(f"unhook all failed: {raw}")
        return raw or "(no hooks to remove)"
    try:
        hid = int(s, 0)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise ValueError("hook_id must be an integer or 'all'") from e
    out = str(MANAGER.call(lambda c: c.unhook(hid))).strip()
    if out.lstrip().lower().startswith(("error", "fail")):
        raise RenefError(f"unhook({hid}) failed: {out}")
    return f"hook #{hid} removed" + (f" ({out})" if out else "")


@mcp.tool()
def renef_watch(seconds: float = 5.0) -> str:
    """Collect hook output for a time window (blocks, exclusive). Install hooks first
    (renef_hook_native / renef_hook_java), then call this to capture their prints.
    seconds capped at 60."""
    _require_session()
    seconds = max(0.5, min(float(seconds), 60.0))

    def _watch(client) -> str:
        return client.watch_collect(seconds)

    text = str(MANAGER.call(_watch)).strip()
    return _truncate(text or f"(no hook output within {seconds}s)")


@mcp.tool()
def renef_backtrace() -> str:
    """Print the current thread call stack in the target process (useful inside hook debugging)."""
    _require_session()
    return _truncate(_run_lua("print(Thread.backtrace())"))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@mcp.tool()
def renef_list_scripts() -> str:
    """List the bundled Renef Lua example scripts (hook demos, tracing,
    flutter_ssl / ssl_unpin / root_bypass bypasses). Load one with renef_get_script
    + renef_load_script, or paste into renef_exec."""
    names = renef_scripts.list_scripts()
    if not names:
        return "(no bundled scripts)"
    return f"{len(names)} scripts:\n" + "\n".join(names)


@mcp.tool()

    """Return the Lua source of a bundled script (see renef_list_scripts), e.g.
    renef_get_script("flutter_ssl")."""
    try:
        content = renef_scripts.get_script_content(name)
    except FileNotFoundError as e:
        raise ValueError(str(e)) from e
    return _truncate(content)

    try:
        text = technique_docs.get_technique(category, platform)
    except ValueError as e:

        return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

        'hook("com/example/Class", "method", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Z", {onEnter=function(args) args.skip=true end})\n'

    )


@mcp.prompt()
def renef_expert(task: str = "hook a function and log its arguments") -> str:
    """Guidance prompt for writing Renef Lua instrumentation scripts."""
    return (
        "You are writing Lua scripts for Renef (Android ARM64 dynamic instrumentation). "
        "You are NOT writing Frida code: no Interceptor.attach, no Java.perform, no NativeFunction.\n\n"
        f"Task: {task}\n\n"
        "Workflow:\n"
        "1. renef_status - verify server + session.\n"
        "2. renef_spawn('<package>') or renef_attach(<pid>).\n"
        "3. Discover with renef_module_list / renef_module_exports('libtarget.so', filter='...') "
        "or renef_exec('print(Module.list())'). NEVER guess offsets.\n"
        "4. Install hooks with renef_hook_native / renef_hook_java, or complex logic via "
        "renef_exec with hook(). Wrap possibly-missing classes in pcall.\n"
        "5. Capture output with renef_watch(seconds) or renef_exec prints.\n"
        "6. Clean up with renef_unhook('all').\n\n"
        "Critical rules: Java class names use slashes (com/example/Class); void-method bypasses "
        "need args.skip=true in onEnter; onLeave may return a new value (integer/boolean/JNI.string); "
        "use print() for all logging; find offsets via Module.exports, never hardcode addresses.\n"
        "Full API reference: read resource renef://docs/lua-api."
    )


def main() -> int:
    mcp.run()  # stdio transport (default)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

`renef_mcp/session.py`:

```py
"""Active target session management.

renef-mcp keeps a single TCP connection to renef_server (the device server is
single-client) and at most one attached target. All access is serialized with
an RLock. The connection is lazily (re)established, so server-level commands
(ping/resume) work without a target too.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from typing import Callable, Optional

from renef_mcp.client import RenefClient, RenefError, validate_package


class NoSessionError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class SessionManager:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._client: Optional[RenefClient] = None
        self._last_action: str = "none"

    @property
    def lock(self) -> threading.RLock:
        return self._lock

    @property
    def pid(self) -> int:
        return self._client.pid if self._client else -1

    @property
    def last_action(self) -> str:
        return self._last_action

    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self._client and self._client.connected() and self._client.pid > 0)

    def client(self) -> RenefClient:
        """Get the (lazily connected) client. Callers must hold the lock."""
        if self._client is None:
            self._client = RenefClient()
        if not self._client.connected():
            self._client.connect()
        return self._client

    def _reset_client(self) -> None:
        if self._client is not None:
            self._client.close()

    def require_target(self) -> RenefClient:
        if not self.is_active():
            raise NoSessionError(
                "No active target. Use renef_attach(pid) or renef_spawn(package) first "
                "(renef_list_processes / renef_list_apps help find targets)."
            )
        return self._client  # type: ignore[return-value]

    def attach(self, pid: int) -> int:
        # Mirror spawn(): validate before _reset_client() so a bad pid does
        # not tear down a healthy live session.
        pid = int(pid)
        if pid <= 0:
            raise ValueError(f"pid must be positive, got {pid}")
        with self._lock:
            self._reset_client()
            client = self.client()
            try:
                new_pid = client.attach(pid)
            except (RenefError, OSError):
                # hard failure poisons this connection (server may be mid-state)
                self._reset_client()
                raise
            self._last_action = f"attach {pid}"
            return new_pid

    def spawn(self, package: str) -> int:
        # Validate before _reset_client(): a bad package must not drop a live
        # session (the server is never touched).
        package = validate_package(package)
        with self._lock:
            self._reset_client()
            client = self.client()
            try:
                new_pid = client.spawn(package)
            except (RenefError, OSError):
                # A validation error (ValueError) happens before the server is
                # touched and must not tear down a healthy active session.
                self._reset_client()
                raise
            self._last_action = f"spawn {package} (gated)"
            return new_pid

    def disconnect(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._reset_client()
            self._last_action = "closed"

    # -- convenience wrappers -------------------------------------------------

    def exec(self, lua: str, total_cap: float = 30.0) -> tuple[bool, str]:
        with self._lock:
            return self.require_target().exec(lua, total_cap=total_cap)

    def call(self, fn: Callable[[RenefClient], object]) -> object:
        """Run fn(client) under the lock (client may or may not have a target)."""
        with self._lock:
            return fn(self.client())

    def with_target(self, fn: Callable[[RenefClient], object]) -> object:
        """Run fn(client) under the lock; requires an attached target."""
        with self._lock:
            return fn(self.require_target())


MANAGER = SessionManager()

```

`renef_mcp/techniques.py`:

```py
"""Vendored technique documentation loader (from ahmeth4n/renef-mcp, MIT).

Currently ships the ssl_pinning knowledge base: platform-mapped markdown that
explains how certificate pinning works per framework (android_native, flutter,
react_native, unity, xamarin, cordova/ionic) and how to bypass it.  Exposed to
agents via the renef_technique_list / renef_technique tools.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

_TECH_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "techniques"

# Keep assembled documents within a sane context budget.
MAX_CHARS = 120_000


def list_categories() -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """Map technique category -> available platforms."""
    out: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for category_dir in sorted(_TECH_DIR.iterdir()):
        mapping = category_dir / "platform_mapping.json"
        if category_dir.is_dir() and mapping.is_file():
            data = json.loads(mapping.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            out[category_dir.name] = sorted(data.get("platforms", {}))
    return out


def get_technique(category: str, platform: str) -> str:
    """Assemble the markdown documents for category+platform.

    Platform names are normalized like upstream: case-insensitive, hyphens to
    underscores, with common aliases (android -> android_native,
    rn/reactnative -> react_native, cordova/ionic -> cordova_ionic).
    """
    categories = list_categories()
    if category not in categories:
        raise ValueError(
            f"unknown technique category {category!r}; "
            f"available: {', '.join(categories)}"
        )
    mapping = json.loads(
        (_TECH_DIR / category / "platform_mapping.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )

    key = platform.strip().lower().replace("-", "_")
    aliases = {
        "android": "android_native",
        "rn": "react_native",
        "reactnative": "react_native",
        "cordova": "cordova_ionic",
        "ionic": "cordova_ionic",
    }
    key = aliases.get(key, key)
    if key not in mapping.get("platforms", {}):
        raise ValueError(
            f"unknown platform {platform!r} for {category}; "
            f"available: {', '.join(sorted(mapping['platforms']))}"
        )

    base = _TECH_DIR / category
    sections = []
    for filename in mapping["platforms"][key].get("files", []):
        path = base / filename
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        sections.append(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))

    text = "\n\n---\n\n".join(sections)
    if len(text) > MAX_CHARS:
        text = text[:MAX_CHARS] + f"\n\n... [truncated, {len(text) - MAX_CHARS} chars dropped]"
    return text

```
