            if (connection.getClass().getName().includes('HttpsURLConnection')) {
                // Connection will use default (non-pinned) SSL
            }
            
            return connection;
        };
    } catch(e) {
        console.log('[-] HurlStack hook failed: ' + e);
    }
});

## 9. Unity

Unity games/apps can implement SSL pinning through C# scripts or native plugins.

### 9.1 How Unity Networking Works

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    C# Scripts                                │
│   UnityWebRequest / WWW / .NET HttpClient                   │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    IL2CPP / Mono                             │
│              Compiled C# code                                │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              Native Platform Layer                           │
│   Android: uses Java network stack via JNI                  │
│   IL2CPP: libil2cpp.so                                      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 9.2 Unity Pinning Implementations

#### Method 1: CertificateHandler Class

```csharp
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Networking;
using System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates;

public class PinnedCertificateHandler : CertificateHandler
{
    // SHA-256 hash of the server's public key
    private static readonly string[] PINS = {
        "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=",
        "BBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBB="
    };
    
    protected override bool ValidateCertificate(byte[] certificateData)
    {
        try
        {
            var cert = new X509Certificate2(certificateData);
            var publicKey = cert.GetPublicKey();
            
            using (var sha256 = System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256.Create())
            {
                var hash = sha256.ComputeHash(publicKey);
                var hashBase64 = System.Convert.ToBase64String(hash);
                
                foreach (var pin in PINS)
                {
                    if (hashBase64 == pin)
                    {
                        Debug.Log("Certificate pin matched!");
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
            
            Debug.LogError("Certificate pinning failed!");
            return false;
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            Debug.LogError("Certificate validation error: " + e.Message);
            return false;
        }
    }
}

// Usage
public class ApiClient : MonoBehaviour
{
    IEnumerator FetchData(string url)
    {
        using (var request = UnityWebRequest.Get(url))
        {
            request.certificateHandler = new PinnedCertificateHandler();
            
            yield return request.SendWebRequest();
            
            if (request.result == UnityWebRequest.Result.Success)
            {
                Debug.Log("Data: " + request.downloadHandler.text);
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.LogError("Request failed: " + request.error);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

#### Method 2: Native Plugin Pinning

```csharp
// C# wrapper
public class NativeSSLPinning
{
    #if UNITY_ANDROID && !UNITY_EDITOR
    private static AndroidJavaObject pinnedClient;
    
    public static void Initialize(string[] pins)
    {
        using (var unityPlayer = new AndroidJavaClass("com.unity3d.player.UnityPlayer"))
        {
            var activity = unityPlayer.GetStatic<AndroidJavaObject>("currentActivity");
            var context = activity.Call<AndroidJavaObject>("getApplicationContext");

                "com.game.ssl.PinnedHttpClient",
                context,
                new AndroidJavaObject("java.util.ArrayList", pins)
            );
        }

    {

    }
    #endif
}

### 9.3 Unity Bypass Techniques

#### Method 1: Hook CertificateHandler (Frida / Renef + IL2CPP)

```javascript
// For IL2CPP builds
function bypassUnityCertHandler() {
    // Find il2cpp
    var il2cpp = Process.findModuleByName('libil2cpp.so');
    if (!il2cpp) {
        console.log('[-] libil2cpp.so not found');
        return;
    }
    
    console.log('[+] libil2cpp.so at: ' + il2cpp.base);
    
    // Search for CertificateHandler::ValidateCertificate
    // This requires finding the method through IL2CPP metadata
    
    // Alternative: Hook at the Java layer
    Java.perform(function() {
        // Unity ultimately uses Android's SSL stack
        var X509TrustManager = Java.use('javax.net.ssl.X509TrustManager');
        
        var BypassTrustManager = Java.registerClass({
            name: 'com.unity.bypass.TrustManager',
            implements: [X509TrustManager],
            methods: {
                checkClientTrusted: function(chain, authType) {},
                checkServerTrusted: function(chain, authType) {},
                getAcceptedIssuers: function() { return []; }
            }
        });
        
        var SSLContext = Java.use('javax.net.ssl.SSLContext');
        SSLContext.init.overload(
            '[Ljavax.net.ssl.KeyManager;',
            '[Ljavax.net.ssl.TrustManager;',
            'java.security.SecureRandom'
        ).implementation = function(km, tm, sr) {
            console.log('[+] Unity SSLContext.init bypassed');
            this.init(km, [BypassTrustManager.$new()], sr);
        };
    });
}

bypassUnityCertHandler();
```

#### Method 2: Patch IL2CPP Binary

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Unity IL2CPP Certificate Handler Patcher
Patches ValidateCertificate to always return true
"""

import sys
import struct

def patch_il2cpp(filename, output):
    with open(filename, 'rb') as f:
        data = bytearray(f.read())
    
    # Search for ValidateCertificate return pattern
    # ARM64: MOV W0, #0 (failure) followed by RET
    # We change to MOV W0, #1 (success)
    
    # Pattern varies by Unity version
    patterns = [
        (b'\x00\x00\x80\x52\xc0\x03\x5f\xd6', 
         b'\x20\x00\x80\x52\xc0\x03\x5f\xd6'),  # MOV W0,#0;RET -> MOV W0,#1;RET
    ]
    
    patched = 0
    for original, replacement in patterns:
        idx = 0
        while True:
            idx = data.find(original, idx)
            if idx == -1:
                break
            data[idx:idx+len(replacement)] = replacement
            patched += 1
            idx += len(replacement)
            print(f'[+] Patched at offset 0x{idx:x}')
    
    if patched > 0:
        with open(output, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(data)
        print(f'[+] Saved patched binary with {patched} patches')
    else:
        print('[-] No patterns found')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) != 3:
        print(f'Usage: {sys.argv[0]} <libil2cpp.so> <output.so>')
        sys.exit(1)
    patch_il2cpp(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2])
```

#### Method 3: Hook via Unity's Mono (for Mono builds)

```javascript
// For Mono builds (not IL2CPP)
Java.perform(function() {
    // Find the CertificateHandler class in Mono
    Java.enumerateLoadedClasses({
        onMatch: function(className) {
            if (className.includes('CertificateHandler')) {
                console.log('[+] Found: ' + className);
                try {
                    var clazz = Java.use(className);
                    // Hook ValidateCertificate
                    if (clazz.ValidateCertificate) {
                        clazz.ValidateCertificate.implementation = function(data) {
                            console.log('[*] Unity CertificateHandler bypassed');
                            return true;
                        };
                    }
                } catch(e) {}
            }
        },
        onComplete: function() {}
    });
});
```

## 7. Xamarin

Xamarin apps can implement SSL pinning through both C# managed code and native platform code.

### 7.1 How Xamarin Networking Works

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    C# / .NET Code                            │
│   HttpClient / WebClient / RestSharp                        │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    Mono Runtime                              │
│              HttpClientHandler / WebRequestHandler           │
└─────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                          │
                          ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              Platform-Specific Handler                       │
│   AndroidClientHandler (uses OkHttp/HttpURLConnection)      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 7.2 Xamarin Pinning Implementations

#### Method 1: ServicePointManager.ServerCertificateValidationCallback

```csharp
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Security;
using System.Security.Cryptography.X509Certificates;

public class PinnedHttpClient
{
    private static readonly string[] PinnedPublicKeys = {
        "sha256/AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=",
        "sha256/BBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBB="
    };
    
    static PinnedHttpClient()
    {
        ServicePointManager.ServerCertificateValidationCallback = 
            ValidateServerCertificate;
    }
    
    private static bool ValidateServerCertificate(
        object sender,
        X509Certificate certificate,
        X509Chain chain,
        SslPolicyErrors sslPolicyErrors)
    {
        // First check for standard SSL errors
        if (sslPolicyErrors != SslPolicyErrors.None)
            return false;
        
        // Then verify pin
        foreach (var chainElement in chain.ChainElements)
        {
            var publicKey = chainElement.Certificate.GetPublicKey();
            var hash = ComputeSha256Hash(publicKey);
            var pin = $"sha256/{Convert.ToBase64String(hash)}";
            
            if (PinnedPublicKeys.Contains(pin))
                return true;
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    private static byte[] ComputeSha256Hash(byte[] data)
    {
        using (var sha256 = SHA256.Create())
        {
            return sha256.ComputeHash(data);
        }
    }
}
```

#### Method 2: Custom HttpClientHandler

```csharp
public class PinningHandler : HttpClientHandler
{
    private readonly string[] _pins;
    
    public PinningHandler(params string[] pins)
    {
        _pins = pins;
        ServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback = ValidateCertificate;
    }
    
    private bool ValidateCertificate(
        HttpRequestMessage request,
        X509Certificate2 certificate,
        X509Chain chain,
        SslPolicyErrors errors)
    {
        if (errors != SslPolicyErrors.None)
            return false;
        
        // Check each certificate in chain
        foreach (var element in chain.ChainElements)
        {
            var cert = element.Certificate;
            using (var sha256 = SHA256.Create())
            {
                var hash = sha256.ComputeHash(cert.GetPublicKey());
                var pin = "sha256/" + Convert.ToBase64String(hash);
                
                if (_pins.Contains(pin))
                    return true;
            }
        }
        
        return false;
    }
}

// Usage
var handler = new PinningHandler(
    "sha256/AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=",
    "sha256/BBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBBB="
);
var client = new HttpClient(handler);
```

#### Method 3: ModernHttpClient with Pinning

```csharp
// Using ModernHttpClient with native handlers
public class NativePinningHandler
{
    public static HttpClient CreatePinnedClient()
    {
        // Uses OkHttp on Android
        var handler = new NativeMessageHandler(
            throwOnCaptiveNetwork: true,
            customSSLVerification: true
        );
        
        // Add certificate validation
        handler.ServerCertificateCustomValidationCallback = 
            (message, cert, chain, errors) =>
            {
                return ValidatePin(cert, chain);
            };
        
        return new HttpClient(handler);
    }
}
```

### 7.3 Xamarin Bypass Techniques

#### Method 1: Hook Mono Runtime (Frida / Renef)

```javascript
Java.perform(function() {
    // Hook Mono's certificate validation
    
    // Find Mono internal methods
    var monoClasses = [];
    Java.enumerateLoadedClasses({
        onMatch: function(className) {
            if (className.includes('mono') || 
                className.includes('Mono') ||
                className.includes('Xamarin')) {
                monoClasses.push(className);
            }
        },
        onComplete: function() {}
    });
    
    console.log('[*] Found Mono classes: ' + monoClasses.length);

            'android.net.http.X509TrustManagerExtensions'

            '[Ljava.security.cert.X509Certificate;',

        };

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/Solution_0x1.md`:

```md
## Prerequisites

- A rooted ARM64 Android device with `adb` access.
- `renef_server` running on the device and `android-renef-mcp` registered in your MCP client.
- The challenge APK installed (`~/github/Frida_Labs/Frida 0x1/Challenge 0x1.apk`).
- Basic jadx/dex skills and small Lua snippets.
- Important: this is Renef, not Frida. There is no `Java.perform`, no `Interceptor`,
  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Getting ready

Start the app through the MCP with a gated spawn so the script runs before the
app's own code:

```
renef_spawn("<package>")
renef_load_script("<path-to-script>")
```

The first `renef_exec` (including `renef_load_script`) automatically resumes the
gated process. Nothing else needs to be attached.

## Challenge 0x1

If you open the app you get an input box and a Submit button. The decompiled
`MainActivity` has `get_random()I` and `check(II)V`. The button handler passes a
random number and your input to `check`, which compares the input with
`random * 2 + 4`.

The Frida solution hooks `get_random()` and returns a known value. On this
device that hook does not fire — the tiny static method gets inlined by ART into
`check`, so the app never calls the method we patched. What does fire is a hook
on `check(II)V`; its second argument is the random number. We log it, work out
the answer, and submit it on a second click.

The Renef template for a method hook is:

```lua
hook("class/name/Here", "methodName", "(signature)V", {
  onEnter = function(args) ... end,
  onLeave = function(retval) ... end,
})
```

Note the Java class uses slashes instead of dots and the full JNI signature is
required. Here is the real script:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x1 — hook a Java method and override its return value.
-- MainActivity.get_random()I supplies the random number passed to check(II)V.
-- check() compares input against random*2+4; forcing 100 means 204 is correct.

-- Renef's agent thread needs the app classloader before Java.use() can resolve
-- app classes (boot classes work without this).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

-- ART inlines the tiny static get_random()I into check(), so hooking that
-- static method does not intercept the UI path. Instead hook check(II)V
-- (an instance method, verified to fire) and log its random argument: the
-- correct input is random*2+4. Re-entering check() from inside the handler
-- crashes the agent on this build, so do not call Java from onEnter.
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    hook('com/ad2001/frida0x1/MainActivity', 'check', '(II)V', {
        onEnter = function(args)
            print('[0x1] check() random=' .. tostring(args[2])
                  .. ', expected input=' .. tostring((tonumber(args[2]) or 0) * 2 + 4))
        end,
    })
end)
if not ok then
    print('[0x1] HOOK FAILED: ' .. tostring(err))
else
    print('[0x1] check hook installed; submit any number to reveal the expected input')
end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0x1`. Script: `challenge_0x1.lua`.

1. Load the script, then type any number and press Submit. The hook prints the
   expected input in the MCP output (use `renef_watch` if you want to see it live).
2. Type the printed number and press Submit again.

The TextView changes to `FRIDA{BABY_HOOK_0x1}`.

One hard-earned rule: do **not** call a Java method from inside a hook handler
on this build. I tried re-entering `check()` from `onEnter` and it killed the
agent process. Two separate clicks are slower but reliable.

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/Solution_0x2.md`:

```md
## Prerequisites

- A rooted ARM64 Android device with `adb` access.
- `renef_server` running on the device and `android-renef-mcp` registered in your MCP client.
- The challenge APK installed (`~/github/Frida_Labs/Frida 0x2/Challenge 0x2.apk`).
- Basic jadx/dex skills and small Lua snippets.
- Important: this is Renef, not Frida. There is no `Java.perform`, no `Interceptor`,
  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Getting ready

Start the app through the MCP with a gated spawn so the script runs before the
app's own code:

```
renef_spawn("<package>")
renef_load_script("<path-to-script>")
```

The first `renef_exec` (including `renef_load_script`) automatically resumes the
gated process. Nothing else needs to be attached.

## Challenge 0x2

The app only shows a `HOOK ME!` TextView and never calls the method we want.
Decompilation shows `MainActivity.get_flag(I)V`: it checks whether the argument
is `4919` and, if so, decrypts a flag into the TextView. Since nothing calls it,
we have to call it ourselves.

Frida does that inside `Java.perform` and does not worry about threads. Renef
runs Lua on its own agent thread; calling an Android UI method from there hangs.
The fix is to post a `Runnable` to the main looper. `Java.registerClass` gives us
a Java class that implements `java/lang/Runnable`, and `Handler.post` runs its
`run()` method on the UI thread.

The class name again uses slashes: `com/ad2001/frida0x2/MainActivity`.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x2: call MainActivity.get_flag(4919) from the UI thread.
-- The method decrypts a flag and sets a TextView, so calling it from the
-- agent's background thread hangs/fails on Android; post a Runnable to the
-- main looper instead.

local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local worker = Java.registerClass({
        implements = {"java/lang/Runnable"},
        methods = {
            run = function()
                local ok2, err2 = pcall(function()
                    Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x2/MainActivity'):call('get_flag', '(I)V', 4919)
                end)
                print('[0x2] get_flag(4919) on UI thread: ' .. tostring(ok2) .. ' ' .. tostring(err2))
            end,
        },
    })
    local looper = Java.use('android/os/Looper'):call('getMainLooper', '()Landroid/os/Looper;')
    local handler = Java.use('android/os/Handler'):new('(Landroid/os/Looper;)V', looper)
    handler:call('post', '(Ljava/lang/Runnable;)Z', worker)
end)
if ok then print('[0x2] runnable posted to main thread') else print('[0x2] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0x2`. Script: `challenge_0x2.lua`.

Reload the app before running this one, then load the script. No interaction is
needed — the posted Runnable calls `get_flag(4919)` by itself and the TextView
turns into `FLAG{BABY_HOOKS_0x2}`.

The `Java.registerClass` pattern is worth remembering; it is also used in the
bundled `ssl_unpin.lua` example for trust managers.

```

## Challenge 0x3

Here the app has a `Checker` class with a static `code` field and an
`increase()` method. The button checks whether `code` reached `512`; if it did,
it shows the flag. Frida simply writes `Checker.code.value = 512`.

Renef has no way to write a static field — `Java.use` gives wrappers with
`new`/`call` only. But `increase()` adds one each time, so calling it 256 times
gets us to the same value. Less elegant, same result.

```lua
-- Shared prelude: teach the agent thread the app classloader so Java.use()
-- resolves app classes (boot classes work without it). Inline this at the top
-- of each challenge script (Renef Lua has no require()).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

-- Frida Labs 0x3: Checker.code must reach 512. Renef cannot write static
-- fields, but Checker.increase() adds one per call: 256 calls == 512.
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local checker = Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x3/Checker')
    for _ = 1, 256 do checker:call('increase', '()V') end
end)
if ok then print('[0x3] increase() called 256 times') else print('[0x3] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0x3`. Script: `challenge_0x3.lua`.

Load the script and press the Click me button. Watch for a Toast and a TextView
reading `FRIDA{INJECT_AND_INSPECT}`.

If you run the script twice without restarting the app, `code` will be 1024 and
the check fails again. Restart between attempts.

```

## Challenge 0x4

There is nothing in `MainActivity`. The interesting class is `Check`, with an
instance method `get_flag(I)Ljava/lang/String;`. It XOR-decodes a string using
the key `15`, but only when the argument is `1337`. The method is never called
by the app.

Frida uses `$new()` and `console.log`. In Renef we use `:new("()V")` and
`print()`. Java strings come back as Lua strings, so concatenation works
directly.

```lua
-- Shared prelude: teach the agent thread the app classloader so Java.use()
-- resolves app classes (boot classes work without it). Inline this at the top
-- of each challenge script (Renef Lua has no require()).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

-- Frida Labs 0x4: instantiate Check and call get_flag(1337). The flag is
-- returned to the caller (Frida console only in the original solution).
local ok, result = pcall(function()
    local check = Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x4/Check'):new('()V')
    return check:call('get_flag', '(I)Ljava/lang/String;', 1337)
end)
if ok and result then print('FLAG ' .. tostring(result)) else print('[0x4] FAIL: ' .. tostring(result)) end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0x4`. Script: `challenge_0x4.lua`.

Spawn the app and load the script. There is no UI change in this challenge —
the flag appears in the MCP output:

```
FLAG FRIDA{XORED_INSTANCE}
```

If `:new()` returns nil, the class has not been loaded yet; click the app once
(or restart it) and run the script again.

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/Solution_0x5.md`:

```md
## Prerequisites

- A rooted ARM64 Android device with `adb` access.
- `renef_server` running on the device and `android-renef-mcp` registered in your MCP client.
- The challenge APK installed (`~/github/Frida_Labs/Frida 0x5/Challenge 0x5.apk`).
- Basic jadx/dex skills and small Lua snippets.
- Important: this is Renef, not Frida. There is no `Java.perform`, no `Interceptor`,
  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Getting ready

Start the app through the MCP with a gated spawn so the script runs before the
app's own code:

```
renef_spawn("<package>")
renef_load_script("<path-to-script>")
```

The first `renef_exec` (including `renef_load_script`) automatically resumes the
gated process. Nothing else needs to be attached.

## Challenge 0x5

The app has a `flag(I)V` method on `MainActivity`, but nothing ever calls it —
exactly the case where Frida's `Java.choose` shines. Renef does not have
`Java.choose`, so we find the live Activity another way.

Every Android app has an `ActivityThread` with an `mActivities` map. Using plain
reflection (`getDeclaredField`, `setAccessible`, `java.lang.reflect.Array`) we
walk that map, find the `MainActivity` instance, locate its declared `flag`
method, and invoke it with an `Integer` wrapper for `1337`.

The reflection helpers are a bit verbose, but they are reusable for the next two
challenges too.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x5: invoke flag(1337) on the EXISTING MainActivity instance.
-- Renef has no Java.choose, so enumerate ActivityThread.mActivities via
-- reflection, pick the MainActivity instance, find its declared `flag`
-- method, and invoke it with an Integer 1337.

local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
    :call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local Array = Java.use('java/lang/reflect/Array')
local function entries(java_array)
    local out = {}
    local n = Array:call('getLength', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)I', java_array)
    for i = 0, n - 1 do
        out[#out + 1] = Array:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)Ljava/lang/Object;', java_array, i)
    end
    return out
end

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local thread = AT:call('currentActivityThread', '()Landroid/app/ActivityThread;')
    local field = thread:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
        :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'mActivities')
    field:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
    local map = field:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', thread)
    local records = entries(map:call('values', '()Ljava/util/Collection;'):call('toArray', '()[Ljava/lang/Object;'))

    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        local af = record:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
            :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'activity')
        af:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
        local activity = af:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', record)
        if activity then
            local class_name = activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;')
            if class_name == 'com.ad2001.frida0x5.MainActivity' then
                local methods = entries(activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
                    :call('getDeclaredMethods', '()[Ljava/lang/reflect/Method;'))
                for _, method in ipairs(methods) do
                    if method:call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;') == 'flag' then
                        method:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
                        local arg = Java.use('java/lang/Integer'):call('valueOf', '(I)Ljava/lang/Integer;', 1337)
                        method:call('invoke', '(Ljava/lang/Object;[Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;',
                            activity, Java.array('java/lang/Object', { arg }))
                        print('[0x5] flag(1337) invoked on the live MainActivity')
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    error('MainActivity/flag not found among activity records')
end)
if not ok then print('[0x5] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0x5`. Script: `challenge_0x5.lua`.

Load the script while the app is in the foreground. It finds the instance and
calls the method; the TextView becomes `FRIDA{ON_MATCH_THIS_INSTANCE}`.

If the app has more than one Activity record (it usually has two), the script
checks the class name instead of guessing an index.

```

  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Challenge 0x6

Same idea as 0x5, but the method takes a `Checker` object. The app creates a
Checker and calls `get_flag(checker)`, but the object's `num1` and `num2` are
wrong. The solution sets them to `1234` and `4321` before calling the method.

Frida uses `Checker.$new()` and direct field writes. Renef has neither field
syntax nor `Java.choose`; we create the object with `:new("()V")` and set the
two ints with `java.lang.reflect.Field:setInt`. Then we invoke `get_flag` on the
live MainActivity with that Checker as the argument.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x6: invoke flag(1337) on the EXISTING MainActivity instance.
-- Renef has no Java.choose, so enumerate ActivityThread.mActivities via
-- reflection, pick the MainActivity instance, find its declared `flag`
-- method, and invoke it with an Integer 1337.

local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
    :call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local Array = Java.use('java/lang/reflect/Array')
local function entries(java_array)
    local out = {}
    local n = Array:call('getLength', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)I', java_array)
    for i = 0, n - 1 do
        out[#out + 1] = Array:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)Ljava/lang/Object;', java_array, i)
    end
    return out
end

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local thread = AT:call('currentActivityThread', '()Landroid/app/ActivityThread;')
    local field = thread:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
        :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'mActivities')
    field:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
    local map = field:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', thread)
    local records = entries(map:call('values', '()Ljava/util/Collection;'):call('toArray', '()[Ljava/lang/Object;'))

    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        local af = record:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
            :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'activity')
        af:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
        local activity = af:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', record)
        if activity then
            local class_name = activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;')
            if class_name == 'com.ad2001.frida0x6.MainActivity' then
                local methods = entries(activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
                    :call('getDeclaredMethods', '()[Ljava/lang/reflect/Method;'))
                for _, method in ipairs(methods) do
                    if method:call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;') == 'get_flag' then
                        method:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
                        local checker = Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x6/Checker'):new('()V')
                        for _, pair in ipairs({{'num1',1234},{'num2',4321}}) do
                            local f = checker:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getDeclaredField',
                                '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', pair[1])
                            f:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
                            f:call('setInt', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)V', checker, pair[2])
                        end
                        method:call('invoke', '(Ljava/lang/Object;[Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;',
                            activity, Java.array('java/lang/Object', { checker }))
                        print('[0x6] flag(1337) invoked on the live MainActivity')
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    error('MainActivity/flag not found among activity records')
end)
if not ok then print('[0x6] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Running it

The script reuses the 0x5 Activity lookup. Load it with the app in the
foreground; the TextView changes to `FRIDA{INSTANCES_AND_INSTANCES}`.

The classes only load once the app has drawn its first screen, so give it a
second or two after the spawn before loading the script.

```

## Challenge 0x7

This is 0x6 with one difference: the Checker has a two-argument constructor.
Frida calls `$new(600, 600)`. The Frida solution also mentions that hooking the
constructor was broken on ARM64, which is another reason to just create the
object ourselves.

In Renef we use `:new("(II)V", 600, 600)` — the constructor signature goes
first, then the arguments — and then invoke `flag(checker)` on the live
MainActivity using the same reflection code as before.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x7: invoke flag(1337) on the EXISTING MainActivity instance.
-- Renef has no Java.choose, so enumerate ActivityThread.mActivities via
-- reflection, pick the MainActivity instance, find its declared `flag`
-- method, and invoke it with an Integer 1337.

Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
    :call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local Array = Java.use('java/lang/reflect/Array')

    for i = 0, n - 1 do
        out[#out + 1] = Array:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)Ljava/lang/Object;', java_array, i)
    end
    return out
end

local ok, err = pcall(function()

    local field = thread:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
        :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'mActivities')
    field:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
    local map = field:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', thread)
    local records = entries(map:call('values', '()Ljava/util/Collection;'):call('toArray', '()[Ljava/lang/Object;'))

    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        local af = record:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
            :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'activity')
        af:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
        local activity = af:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', record)
        if activity then
            local class_name = activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;')
            if class_name == 'com.ad2001.frida0x7.MainActivity' then
                local methods = entries(activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
                    :call('getDeclaredMethods', '()[Ljava/lang/reflect/Method;'))

                        method:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)

                            activity, Java.array('java/lang/Object', { checker }))
                        print('[0x7] flag(1337) invoked on the live MainActivity')
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    error('MainActivity/flag not found among activity records')
end)
if not ok then print('[0x7] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Challenge 0x8

This one is native. The input you type is passed to `cmpstr` in
`libfrida0x8.so`; returning 1 makes the app accept it. The library is loaded
when you first press Submit, not at startup.

Frida resolves the export with `Module.findExportByName`. In Renef we list
exports of a loaded module with `Module.exports`. From the arm64 library:

```
Java_com_ad2001_frida0x8_MainActivity_cmpstr   @ 0x864
```

Renef hooks on not-yet-loaded libraries are deferred automatically, so we can
install the hook before the first click. `onEnter` reads the argument with
`Memory.readString(args[0])`, and `onLeave` returns 1.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x8: cmpstr(Ljava/lang/String;)I is a native method in
-- libfrida0x8.so. The app loads the library on first submit. Hook its JNI
-- export (offset 0x864, verified with readelf) and force return 1.

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    hook('libfrida0x8.so', 0x864, {
        onEnter = function(args)
            local p = Memory.readString(args[0])
            print('[0x8] cmpstr(' .. tostring(p) .. ')')
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print('[0x8] forcing cmpstr()=1 (was ' .. tostring(retval) .. ')')
            return 1
        end,
    })
end)
if ok then print('[0x8] cmpstr hook installed (deferred until lib loads)')
else print('[0x8] HOOK FAILED: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0x8`. Script: `challenge_0x8.lua`.

Type something and press Submit. Android sometimes shows a translate popup
over the input; dismiss it and press Submit again. The hook prints the call and
forces the return value. A screenshot of the app shows
`YEY YOU GOT THE FLAG candidate` — the flag here is your input.

The dump of the argument looks like garbage because the JNI bridge passes a
`jstring` object rather than a `char*`; that is expected and harmless.

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/Solution_0x9.md`:

```md
## Prerequisites

- A rooted ARM64 Android device with `adb` access.
- `renef_server` running on the device and `android-renef-mcp` registered in your MCP client.
- The challenge APK installed (`~/github/Frida_Labs/Frida 0x9/Challenge 0x9.apk`).
- Basic jadx/dex skills and small Lua snippets.
- Important: this is Renef, not Frida. There is no `Java.perform`, no `Interceptor`,
  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Getting ready

Start the app through the MCP with a gated spawn so the script runs before the
app's own code:

```
renef_spawn("<package>")
renef_load_script("<path-to-script>")
```

The first `renef_exec` (including `renef_load_script`) automatically resumes the
gated process. Nothing else needs to be attached.

## Challenge 0x9

Note the package name is `com.ad2001.a0x9`, not `frida0x9`. Pressing CLICK ME
calls a native `check_flag()I` which returns 1. The Frida solution attaches to
the first export and replaces the return value with 1337.

Renef's native hooks work the same way: `hook(lib, offset, {...})` with an
`onLeave` that returns an integer. The arm64 symbol is:

```
Java_com_ad2001_a0x9_MainActivity_check_1flag  @ 0x614
```

The bundled `dere_0x9.lua` example uses exactly this pattern; this script is a
slightly more verbose version with logging.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x9: native check_flag()I in liba0x9.so (first export /
-- Java_com_ad2001_a0x9_MainActivity_check_1flag @ 0x614). Renef defers
-- native hooks on not-yet-loaded libraries until they map.

local lib = 'liba0x9.so'
local base = Module.find(lib)
if not base then
    print('[0x9] ' .. lib .. ' not loaded yet; hook is deferred until it is')
end
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    hook(lib, 0x614, {
        onEnter = function(args)
            print('[0x9] check_flag called')
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print('[0x9] original retval=' .. tostring(retval) .. ', forcing 1337')
            return 1337
        end,
    })
end)
if ok then print('[0x9] check_flag hook installed')
else print('[0x9] HOOK FAILED: ' .. tostring(err)) end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.a0x9`. Script: `challenge_0x9.lua`.

Spawn the app, load the script, press CLICK ME. The hook prints the original
return value and the forced one, and a Toast appears:
`You won FRIDA{NATIVE_LAND_0x2}`.

If the hook is installed while the library is still loading, Renef defers it;
you will see a note about that in the output.

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/Solution_0xA.md`:

```md
## Prerequisites

- A rooted ARM64 Android device with `adb` access.
- `renef_server` running on the device and `android-renef-mcp` registered in your MCP client.
- The challenge APK installed (`~/github/Frida_Labs/Frida 0xA/Challenge 0xA.apk`).
- Basic jadx/dex skills and small Lua snippets.
- Important: this is Renef, not Frida. There is no `Java.perform`, no `Interceptor`,
  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Getting ready

Start the app through the MCP with a gated spawn so the script runs before the
app's own code:

```
renef_spawn("<package>")
renef_load_script("<path-to-script>")
```

The first `renef_exec` (including `renef_load_script`) automatically resumes the
gated process. Nothing else needs to be attached.

## Challenge 0xA

The flag is produced by a native function `_Z8get_flagii` in
`libfrida0xa.so`. Frida calls it with `new NativeFunction(base.add(0x18BB0),
'void', ['int','int'])` and passes `(1, 2)` because the function checks that the
sum is 3.

Renef has no `NativeFunction`, so there is nothing to call from Lua. What the
app *does* call is `stringFromJNI()`, whose native entry is at `0x1dbe0`. We
patch its first twelve bytes with three ARM64 instructions:

```
mov w0, #1   ; 20 00 80 52
mov w1, #2   ; 41 00 80 52
b  0x1dd60  ; 5e 00 00 14   (branch to get_flag from 0x1dbe8)
```

`Memory.patch` handles the page protection for us. The next time the app calls
`stringFromJNI()`, it jumps into `get_flag(1,2)` instead.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0xA: Renef has no NativeFunction, so instead of calling
-- _Z8get_flagii directly, patch the entry of stringFromJNI() (which the app
-- actually calls) to set w0=1, w1=2 and branch to get_flag@0x1dd60.
--   mov w0, #1   -> 20 00 80 52
--   mov w1, #2   -> 41 00 80 52
--   b 0x1dd60    -> 5e 00 00 14  (from 0x1dbe8: (0x1dd60-0x1dbe8)/4 = 0x5e)
-- get_flag(1,2) sums to 3 and logs the decoded flag with logcat tag FLAG.

local lib = 'libfrida0xa.so'
local base = Module.find(lib)
if not base then
    print('[0xA] ' .. lib .. ' not loaded yet; reload after the app draws')
    return
end

local target = base + 0x1dbe0
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    Memory.patch(target, '\x20\x00\x80\x52\x41\x00\x80\x52\x5e\x00\x00\x14')
end)
if not ok then
    print('[0xA] PATCH FAILED: ' .. tostring(err))
else
    local bytes = Memory.read(target, 12)
    local hex = ''
    for i = 1, 12 do hex = hex .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(bytes, i)) end
    print('[0xA] stringFromJNI entry patched: ' .. hex)
end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0xa`. Script: `challenge_0xA.lua`.

Load the script, then bring the app back to the foreground (an `am start` of
the activity is enough). Check logcat:

```
D FLAG    : Decrypted Flag: FRIDA{DONT_CALL_ME}
```

The patched function does not return a proper jstring any more, so the app's
TextView may show nothing. That is fine for this challenge — we only need the
flag in the log.

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/Solution_0xB.md`:

```md
## Prerequisites

- A rooted ARM64 Android device with `adb` access.
- `renef_server` running on the device and `android-renef-mcp` registered in your MCP client.
- The challenge APK installed (`~/github/Frida_Labs/Frida 0xB/Challenge 0xB.apk`).
- Basic jadx/dex skills and small Lua snippets.
- Important: this is Renef, not Frida. There is no `Java.perform`, no `Interceptor`,
  and no `NativeFunction`. The scripts below use the Renef Lua API instead.

## Getting ready

Start the app through the MCP with a gated spawn so the script runs before the
app's own code:

```
renef_spawn("<package>")
renef_load_script("<path-to-script>")
```

The first `renef_exec` (including `renef_load_script`) automatically resumes the
gated process. Nothing else needs to be attached.

## Challenge 0xB

The last one is about patching a branch. `getFlag()` in `libfrida0xb.so`
compares a local variable with `0x539` (1337). The comparison always fails, so
a `b.ne` at `0x15248` jumps past the flag decoder. Frida replaces that branch
with NOPs using `Arm64Writer` / `Memory.patchCode`.

Renef has a simpler `Memory.patch` that already deals with memory protection.
One NOP is enough on arm64 because the branch is a single 4-byte instruction:

```
54 07 00 54   (b.ne)   ->   1f 20 03 d5   (nop)
```

The library is loaded when you press CLICK ME, so click once first, then load
the script, then click again.

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0xB: getFlag() native code in libfrida0xb.so has a conditional
-- branch b.ne at 0x15248 (bytes 21 07 00 54) that skips the flag decoder.
-- Replace it with an ARM64 NOP (1f 20 03 d5) so execution falls through.

local lib = 'libfrida0xb.so'
local base = Module.find(lib)
if not base then
    print('[0xB] ' .. lib .. ' not loaded; tap CLICK ME once, then reload this script')
    return
end
local target = base + 0x15248
local before = Memory.read(target, 4)
local hex = ''
for i = 1, 4 do hex = hex .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(before, i)) end
local ok, err = pcall(function() Memory.patch(target, '\x1f\x20\x03\xd5') end)
if not ok then
    print('[0xB] PATCH FAILED: ' .. tostring(err))
else
    local after = Memory.read(target, 4)
    local hex2 = ''
    for i = 1, 4 do hex2 = hex2 .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(after, i)) end
    print('[0xB] patched b.ne @ 0x15248: ' .. hex .. ' -> ' .. hex2)
end
```

## Running it

Package: `com.ad2001.frida0xb`. Script: `challenge_0xB.lua`.

After the second click, logcat shows the flag:

```
D FLAG :  : FRIDA{NATIVE_HACKER}
```

The script prints the bytes before and after the patch; `21070054 ->
1f2003d5` means it worked.

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/_prelude.lua`:

-- Shared prelude: teach the agent thread the app classloader so Java.use()
-- resolves app classes (boot classes work without it). Inline this at the top
-- of each challenge script (Renef Lua has no require()).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x1.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x1 — hook a Java method and override its return value.
-- MainActivity.get_random()I supplies the random number passed to check(II)V.
-- check() compares input against random*2+4; forcing 100 means 204 is correct.

-- Renef's agent thread needs the app classloader before Java.use() can resolve
-- app classes (boot classes work without this).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

-- ART inlines the tiny static get_random()I into check(), so hooking that
-- static method does not intercept the UI path. Instead hook check(II)V
-- (an instance method, verified to fire) and log its random argument: the
-- correct input is random*2+4. Re-entering check() from inside the handler
-- crashes the agent on this build, so do not call Java from onEnter.
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    hook('com/ad2001/frida0x1/MainActivity', 'check', '(II)V', {
        onEnter = function(args)
            print('[0x1] check() random=' .. tostring(args[2])
                  .. ', expected input=' .. tostring((tonumber(args[2]) or 0) * 2 + 4))
        end,
    })
end)
if not ok then
    print('[0x1] HOOK FAILED: ' .. tostring(err))
else
    print('[0x1] check hook installed; submit any number to reveal the expected input')
end

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x2.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x2: call MainActivity.get_flag(4919) from the UI thread.
-- The method decrypts a flag and sets a TextView, so calling it from the
-- agent's background thread hangs/fails on Android; post a Runnable to the
-- main looper instead.

local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local worker = Java.registerClass({
        implements = {"java/lang/Runnable"},
        methods = {
            run = function()
                local ok2, err2 = pcall(function()
                    Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x2/MainActivity'):call('get_flag', '(I)V', 4919)
                end)
                print('[0x2] get_flag(4919) on UI thread: ' .. tostring(ok2) .. ' ' .. tostring(err2))
            end,
        },
    })
    local looper = Java.use('android/os/Looper'):call('getMainLooper', '()Landroid/os/Looper;')
    local handler = Java.use('android/os/Handler'):new('(Landroid/os/Looper;)V', looper)
    handler:call('post', '(Ljava/lang/Runnable;)Z', worker)
end)
if ok then print('[0x2] runnable posted to main thread') else print('[0x2] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x3.lua`:

```lua
-- Shared prelude: teach the agent thread the app classloader so Java.use()
-- resolves app classes (boot classes work without it). Inline this at the top
-- of each challenge script (Renef Lua has no require()).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

-- Frida Labs 0x3: Checker.code must reach 512. Renef cannot write static
-- fields, but Checker.increase() adds one per call: 256 calls == 512.
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local checker = Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x3/Checker')
    for _ = 1, 256 do checker:call('increase', '()V') end
end)
if ok then print('[0x3] increase() called 256 times') else print('[0x3] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x4.lua`:

```lua
-- Shared prelude: teach the agent thread the app classloader so Java.use()
-- resolves app classes (boot classes work without it). Inline this at the top
-- of each challenge script (Renef Lua has no require()).
local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
local t = Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
t:call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

-- Frida Labs 0x4: instantiate Check and call get_flag(1337). The flag is
-- returned to the caller (Frida console only in the original solution).
local ok, result = pcall(function()
    local check = Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x4/Check'):new('()V')
    return check:call('get_flag', '(I)Ljava/lang/String;', 1337)
end)
if ok and result then print('FLAG ' .. tostring(result)) else print('[0x4] FAIL: ' .. tostring(result)) end

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x5.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x5: invoke flag(1337) on the EXISTING MainActivity instance.
-- Renef has no Java.choose, so enumerate ActivityThread.mActivities via
-- reflection, pick the MainActivity instance, find its declared `flag`
-- method, and invoke it with an Integer 1337.

local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
    :call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local Array = Java.use('java/lang/reflect/Array')
local function entries(java_array)
    local out = {}
    local n = Array:call('getLength', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)I', java_array)
    for i = 0, n - 1 do
        out[#out + 1] = Array:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)Ljava/lang/Object;', java_array, i)
    end
    return out
end

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local thread = AT:call('currentActivityThread', '()Landroid/app/ActivityThread;')
    local field = thread:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
        :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'mActivities')
    field:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
    local map = field:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', thread)
    local records = entries(map:call('values', '()Ljava/util/Collection;'):call('toArray', '()[Ljava/lang/Object;'))

    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        local af = record:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
            :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'activity')
        af:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
        local activity = af:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', record)
        if activity then
            local class_name = activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;')
            if class_name == 'com.ad2001.frida0x5.MainActivity' then
                local methods = entries(activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
                    :call('getDeclaredMethods', '()[Ljava/lang/reflect/Method;'))
                for _, method in ipairs(methods) do
                    if method:call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;') == 'flag' then
                        method:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
                        local arg = Java.use('java/lang/Integer'):call('valueOf', '(I)Ljava/lang/Integer;', 1337)
                        method:call('invoke', '(Ljava/lang/Object;[Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;',
                            activity, Java.array('java/lang/Object', { arg }))
                        print('[0x5] flag(1337) invoked on the live MainActivity')
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    error('MainActivity/flag not found among activity records')
end)
if not ok then print('[0x5] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x6.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x6: invoke flag(1337) on the EXISTING MainActivity instance.
-- Renef has no Java.choose, so enumerate ActivityThread.mActivities via
-- reflection, pick the MainActivity instance, find its declared `flag`
-- method, and invoke it with an Integer 1337.

local AT = Java.use('android/app/ActivityThread')
local app = AT:call('currentApplication', '()Landroid/app/Application;')
local cl = app:call('getClassLoader', '()Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;')
Java.use('java/lang/Thread'):call('currentThread', '()Ljava/lang/Thread;')
    :call('setContextClassLoader', '(Ljava/lang/ClassLoader;)V', cl)

local Array = Java.use('java/lang/reflect/Array')
local function entries(java_array)
    local out = {}
    local n = Array:call('getLength', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)I', java_array)
    for i = 0, n - 1 do
        out[#out + 1] = Array:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)Ljava/lang/Object;', java_array, i)
    end
    return out
end

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local thread = AT:call('currentActivityThread', '()Landroid/app/ActivityThread;')
    local field = thread:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
        :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'mActivities')
    field:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
    local map = field:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', thread)
    local records = entries(map:call('values', '()Ljava/util/Collection;'):call('toArray', '()[Ljava/lang/Object;'))

    for _, record in ipairs(records) do
        local af = record:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
            :call('getDeclaredField', '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', 'activity')
        af:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
        local activity = af:call('get', '(Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;', record)
        if activity then
            local class_name = activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;')
            if class_name == 'com.ad2001.frida0x6.MainActivity' then
                local methods = entries(activity:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;')
                    :call('getDeclaredMethods', '()[Ljava/lang/reflect/Method;'))
                for _, method in ipairs(methods) do
                    if method:call('getName', '()Ljava/lang/String;') == 'get_flag' then
                        method:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
                        local checker = Java.use('com/ad2001/frida0x6/Checker'):new('()V')
                        for _, pair in ipairs({{'num1',1234},{'num2',4321}}) do
                            local f = checker:call('getClass', '()Ljava/lang/Class;'):call('getDeclaredField',
                                '(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/reflect/Field;', pair[1])
                            f:call('setAccessible', '(Z)V', true)
                            f:call('setInt', '(Ljava/lang/Object;I)V', checker, pair[2])
                        end
                        method:call('invoke', '(Ljava/lang/Object;[Ljava/lang/Object;)Ljava/lang/Object;',
                            activity, Java.array('java/lang/Object', { checker }))
                        print('[0x6] flag(1337) invoked on the live MainActivity')
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    error('MainActivity/flag not found among activity records')
end)
if not ok then print('[0x6] FAIL: ' .. tostring(err)) end

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0x8.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0x8: cmpstr(Ljava/lang/String;)I is a native method in
-- libfrida0x8.so. The app loads the library on first submit. Hook its JNI
-- export (offset 0x864, verified with readelf) and force return 1.

local ok, err = pcall(function()
    hook('libfrida0x8.so', 0x864, {
        onEnter = function(args)
            local p = Memory.readString(args[0])
            print('[0x8] cmpstr(' .. tostring(p) .. ')')
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print('[0x8] forcing cmpstr()=1 (was ' .. tostring(retval) .. ')')
            return 1
        end,
    })
end)
if ok then print('[0x8] cmpstr hook installed (deferred until lib loads)')
else print('[0x8] HOOK FAILED: ' .. tostring(err)) end

```

-- Frida Labs 0x9: native check_flag()I in liba0x9.so (first export /
-- Java_com_ad2001_a0x9_MainActivity_check_1flag @ 0x614). Renef defers

            print('[0x9] check_flag called')
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print('[0x9] original retval=' .. tostring(retval) .. ', forcing 1337')
            return 1337
        end,
    })
end)

else print('[0x9] HOOK FAILED: ' .. tostring(err)) end

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0xA.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0xA: Renef has no NativeFunction, so instead of calling
-- _Z8get_flagii directly, patch the entry of stringFromJNI() (which the app
-- actually calls) to set w0=1, w1=2 and branch to get_flag@0x1dd60.
--   mov w0, #1   -> 20 00 80 52
--   mov w1, #2   -> 41 00 80 52
--   b 0x1dd60    -> 5e 00 00 14  (from 0x1dbe8: (0x1dd60-0x1dbe8)/4 = 0x5e)
-- get_flag(1,2) sums to 3 and logs the decoded flag with logcat tag FLAG.

local lib = 'libfrida0xa.so'
local base = Module.find(lib)
if not base then
    print('[0xA] ' .. lib .. ' not loaded yet; reload after the app draws')
    return
end

local target = base + 0x1dbe0
local ok, err = pcall(function()
    Memory.patch(target, '\x20\x00\x80\x52\x41\x00\x80\x52\x5e\x00\x00\x14')
end)
if not ok then
    print('[0xA] PATCH FAILED: ' .. tostring(err))
else
    local bytes = Memory.read(target, 12)
    local hex = ''
    for i = 1, 12 do hex = hex .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(bytes, i)) end
    print('[0xA] stringFromJNI entry patched: ' .. hex)
end

```

`scripts/Frida_Labs_Examples/challenge_0xB.lua`:

```lua
-- Frida Labs 0xB: getFlag() native code in libfrida0xb.so has a conditional
-- branch b.ne at 0x15248 (bytes 21 07 00 54) that skips the flag decoder.
-- Replace it with an ARM64 NOP (1f 20 03 d5) so execution falls through.

local lib = 'libfrida0xb.so'
local base = Module.find(lib)
if not base then
    print('[0xB] ' .. lib .. ' not loaded; tap CLICK ME once, then reload this script')
    return
end
local target = base + 0x15248
local before = Memory.read(target, 4)
local hex = ''
for i = 1, 4 do hex = hex .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(before, i)) end
local ok, err = pcall(function() Memory.patch(target, '\x1f\x20\x03\xd5') end)
if not ok then
    print('[0xB] PATCH FAILED: ' .. tostring(err))
else
    local after = Memory.read(target, 4)
    local hex2 = ''
    for i = 1, 4 do hex2 = hex2 .. string.format('%02x', string.byte(after, i)) end
    print('[0xB] patched b.ne @ 0x15248: ' .. hex .. ' -> ' .. hex2)
end

```

`scripts/examples/dere_0x9.lua`:

```lua
-- Hook liba0x9.so first export and return 1337

local lib_name = "liba0x9.so"

print("Looking for " .. lib_name .. "...")

-- Check if library is already loaded
if not Module.find(lib_name) then
    print("[WARN] Library not loaded yet")
    print("[INFO] Trigger the function in the app first, then reload this script")
    return
end

print("Library found! Getting exports...")

local exports = Module.exports(lib_name)
if not exports or #exports == 0 then
    print("[ERROR] No exports in " .. lib_name)
    return
end

-- Hook first export
local func = exports[1]
print("Hooking: " .. func.name .. " @ 0x" .. string.format("%x", func.offset))

hook(lib_name, func.offset, {
    onEnter = function(args)
        print("[CALLED] " .. func.name)
    end,
    onLeave = function(retval)
        print("Original retval: " .. retval)
        return 1337
    end
})

print("Hook installed! Return value will be 1337")

```

`scripts/examples/flutter_ssl.lua`:

```lua
-- Flutter SSL Pinning Bypass for RENEF
-- Works for Flutter apps using BoringSSL

print("[*] Flutter SSL Pinning Bypass loading...")

-- Hardcoded offset for ssl_crypto_x509_session_verify_cert_chain
-- This offset may vary per Flutter version - update if needed
local SSL_VERIFY_OFFSET = 0x5dc730

local bypass_installed = false

-- Function to install SSL bypass on libflutter.so
local function install_ssl_bypass()
    if bypass_installed then
        return true
    end

    local flutter_base = Module.find("libflutter.so")
    if not flutter_base then
        return false
    end

    print(string.format("[+] libflutter.so found at: 0x%x", flutter_base))
    print(string.format("[+] Installing hook at offset: 0x%x", SSL_VERIFY_OFFSET))

    -- Install hook to bypass SSL verification
    hook("libflutter.so", SSL_VERIFY_OFFSET, {
        onEnter = function(args)
            print("[*] SSL verify called!")
        end,
        onLeave = function(retval)
            print("[*] SSL verify bypassing, returning 1")
            return 1  -- Return success (1 = verified)
        end
    })

    bypass_installed = true
    print("[+] SSL pinning bypass ACTIVE!")
    return true
end

-- Try to install bypass immediately if libflutter is already loaded
if install_ssl_bypass() then
    print("[+] Bypass installed on existing libflutter.so")
else
    print("[*] libflutter.so not loaded yet, hooking linker...")

    -- Hook android_dlopen_ext which is used to load libraries
    local linker_name = "linker64"
    local linker_base = Module.find(linker_name)

    if not linker_base then
        print("[-] linker64 not found, trying linker")
        linker_name = "linker"
        linker_base = Module.find(linker_name)
    end

    if not linker_base then
        print("[-] Cannot find linker!")
    else
        print(string.format("[+] %s found at: 0x%x", linker_name, linker_base))

        -- Get linker symbols
        local linker_symbols = Module.symbols(linker_name)
        if not linker_symbols then
            print("[-] Cannot get linker symbols (may be stripped)")
            print("[*] Trying exports instead...")
            linker_symbols = Module.exports(linker_name)
        end

        if linker_symbols then
            -- Find __dl__Z9do_dlopenPKciPK17android_dlextinfoPKv or similar
            local dlopen_offset = nil
            for _, sym in ipairs(linker_symbols) do
                if sym.name:find("do_dlopen") then
                    dlopen_offset = sym.offset
                    print(string.format("[+] Found %s at offset 0x%x", sym.name, sym.offset))
                    break
                end
            end

            if dlopen_offset then
                print(string.format("[+] Hooking %s + 0x%x", linker_name, dlopen_offset))

                hook(linker_name, dlopen_offset, {
                    onEnter = function(args)
                        -- args[0] is the library path
                        local path = Memory.readString(args[0])
                        if path and path:find("libflutter") then
                            print("[+] libflutter.so loading: " .. path)
                        end
                    end,
                    onLeave = function(retval)
                        -- After library loads, try to install bypass
                        if not bypass_installed then
                            install_ssl_bypass()
                        end
                    end
                })
                print("[+] Linker hook installed, waiting for libflutter.so...")
            else
                print("[-] do_dlopen not found in linker symbols")
            end
        else
            print("[-] Cannot get linker symbols/exports")
        end
    end
end

print("[+] Flutter SSL Bypass script loaded")

```

`scripts/examples/java_api_example.lua`:

```lua
-- Java API Test Script
-- Usage: spawn com.example.app && l scripts/test_java_api.lua

print("=== Java API Test ===")

-- Test 1: Load class with Java.use 
print("\n[1] Testing Java.use()...")
local Build = Java.use("android/os/Build")
if Build then
    print("  OK: android.os.Build loaded")
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load Build class")
    return
end

-- Test 2: Static method (non-argument)
print("\n[2] Testing static method call...")
local System = Java.use("java/lang/System")
if System then
    local time = System:call("currentTimeMillis", "()J")
    print(string.format("  System.currentTimeMillis() = %d", time or 0))
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load System class")
end

-- Test 3: Static method (with argument)
print("\n[3] Testing static method with args...")
local String = Java.use("java/lang/String")
if String then
    local str = String:call("valueOf", "(I)Ljava/lang/String;", 123)
    print(string.format("  String.valueOf(123) = %s", tostring(str)))

    local str2 = String:call("valueOf", "(Z)Ljava/lang/String;", true)
    print(string.format("  String.valueOf(true) = %s", tostring(str2)))
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load String class")
end

-- Test 4: Instance creation + instance method
print("\n[4] Testing instance creation and methods...")
local StringBuilder = Java.use("java/lang/StringBuilder")
if StringBuilder then
    local sb = StringBuilder:new("()V")
    if sb then
        print("  OK: StringBuilder instance created")

        sb:call("append", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/StringBuilder;", "Hello")
        sb:call("append", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/StringBuilder;", " World")
        local result = sb:call("toString", "()Ljava/lang/String;")
        print(string.format("  StringBuilder result: %s", tostring(result)))
    else
        print("  FAIL: Could not create StringBuilder instance")
    end
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load StringBuilder class")
end

-- Test 5: Android Context
print("\n[5] Testing Android APIs...")
local ActivityThread = Java.use("android/app/ActivityThread")
if ActivityThread then
    local app = ActivityThread:call("currentApplication", "()Landroid/app/Application;")
    if app then
        local pkgName = app:call("getPackageName", "()Ljava/lang/String;")
        print(string.format("  Package: %s", tostring(pkgName)))
    else
        print("  INFO: No Application context")
    end
else
    print("  FAIL: Could not load ActivityThread")
end

print("\n=== Test Complete ===")

```

`scripts/examples/java_hook.lua`:

```lua
-- Test script for Java String return values
-- Target: com.example.reneftestapp

print("[*] Starting Java String hook test...")

-- Hook childHook - modify return value
hook("com/example/reneftestapp/MainActivity", "childHook", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/String;", {
    onEnter = function(args)
        print("[+] childHook called")
        print("    this = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[1]))
        print("    param (jstring ref) = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[2]))
    end,
    onLeave = function(retval)
        print("[+] childHook onLeave")
        print("    Original: \"" .. (retval.value or "nil") .. "\"")

        -- Modify the return value!
        local newValue = "HOOKED BY RENEF!"
        print("    Replacing with: \"" .. newValue .. "\"")

        return JNI.string(newValue)
    end
})

-- Hook parentHook - just observe
hook("com/example/reneftestapp/MainActivity", "parentHook", "(Ljava/lang/String;)Ljava/lang/String;", {
    onEnter = function(args)
        print("[+] parentHook called")
        print("    this = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[1]))
        print("    param (jstring ref) = " .. string.format("0x%x", args[2]))
    end,
    onLeave = function(retval)
        print("[+] parentHook onLeave")
        print("    Return (raw ref): " .. string.format("0x%x", retval.raw))
        if retval.value then
            print("    Return (string): \"" .. retval.value .. "\"")
        end
        return retval.raw
    end
})

print("[+] Hooks installed!")
print("[*] childHook return value will be replaced with 'HOOKED BY RENEF!'")

```

`scripts/examples/kcov_test.lua`:

```lua
-- KCov Test Script
-- After injecting into Renef: l scripts/examples/kcov_test.lua
--
-- Requirement: kernel CONFIG_KCOV=y
-- Check: adb shell "ls /sys/kernel/debug/kcov"

print(CYAN .. "=== KCov Test ===" .. RESET)

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 1: Can it be opened?
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[1] KCov.open() test...")

local ok, cov = pcall(KCov.open)
if not ok then
    print(RED .. "  FAIL: " .. tostring(cov) .. RESET)
    print(RED .. "  Kernel does not support CONFIG_KCOV=y." .. RESET)
    print(YELLOW .. "  Check: adb shell ls /sys/kernel/debug/kcov" .. RESET)
    return
end
print(GREEN .. "  OK: KCov opened" .. RESET)

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 2: enable/disable cycle
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[2] enable/disable test...")

cov:reset()
cov:enable()

-- make a simple syscall to trigger kernel paths
local f = io.open("/proc/self/maps", "r")
if f then
    local _ = f:read(100)
    f:close()
end

cov:disable()

local count = cov:count()
print(string.format("  Hit: %d kernel functions", count))

if count > 0 then
    print(GREEN .. "  OK: Coverage recorded" .. RESET)
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  WARN: 0 hits - KCOV may not be active" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 3: collect() - PC addresses
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[3] collect() test...")

local pcs = cov:collect(20) -- first 20 entries
print(string.format("  Total: %d PCs, showing first %d:", count, #pcs))

for i, pc in ipairs(pcs) do
    print(string.format("    [%2d] 0x%x", i, pc))
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 4: reset + new measurement
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[4] reset + new measurement test...")

cov:reset()
assert(cov:count() == 0, "count should be 0 after reset")
print("  reset OK (count=0)")

cov:enable()

-- different syscall
os.time()

cov:disable()

local count2 = cov:count()
print(string.format("  New measurement: %d hits", count2))

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 5: edges() - coverage-guided fuzzing
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[5] edges() test...")

-- measure baseline
cov:reset()
cov:enable()
local f1 = io.open("/proc/self/status", "r")
if f1 then f1:read("*a"); f1:close() end
cov:disable()

local edges1 = cov:edges()
local edge_count = 0
for _ in pairs(edges1) do edge_count = edge_count + 1 end
print(string.format("  Baseline: %d unique edges", edge_count))

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 6: diff() - new path detection
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[6] diff() test...")

-- try a different path
cov:reset()
cov:enable()
local f2 = io.open("/proc/self/cmdline", "r")
if f2 then f2:read("*a"); f2:close() end
cov:disable()

local new_edges = cov:diff(edges1)
print(string.format("  New edges: %d", new_edges))

if new_edges > 0 then
    print(GREEN .. "  OK: Different syscall = different kernel path" .. RESET)
else
    print(YELLOW .. "  INFO: Same path may have been used" .. RESET)
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Test 7: syscall mutation + kcov combined
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[7] Syscall mutation + KCov combined test...")

-- This test uses kcov to compare coverage across different inputs
-- Miniature version of a real fuzzing loop

local results = {}

-- try 3 different mutated inputs
local test_fds = {"/proc/self/maps", "/proc/self/status", "/proc/self/stat"}

for i, path in ipairs(test_fds) do
    cov:reset()
    cov:enable()

    local fh = io.open(path, "r")
    if fh then fh:read("*a"); fh:close() end

    cov:disable()

    local c = cov:count()
    local e = cov:edges()
    local ne = 0
    for _ in pairs(e) do ne = ne + 1 end

    results[i] = {path=path, hits=c, edges=ne}
    print(string.format("  Input %d: %s -> %d hits, %d edges", i, path, c, ne))
end

-----------------------------------------------
-- Cleanup
-----------------------------------------------
print("\n[8] Cleanup...")
cov:close()
print(GREEN .. "  KCov closed" .. RESET)

-----------------------------------------------
-- Summary
-----------------------------------------------
print(CYAN .. "\n=== Test Complete ===" .. RESET)
print(string.format("  Test 1 (open):    %s", cov and "PASS" or "FAIL"))
print(string.format("  Test 2 (enable):  %s", count > 0 and "PASS" or "WARN"))
print(string.format("  Test 3 (collect): %s", #pcs > 0 and "PASS" or "WARN"))
print(string.format("  Test 5 (edges):   %s", edge_count > 0 and "PASS" or "WARN"))

```

`scripts/examples/memory_scan.lua`:

```lua
local RED = "\27[31m"
local GREEN = "\27[32m"
local YELLOW = "\27[33m"
local BLUE = "\27[34m"
local CYAN = "\27[36m"
local RESET = "\27[0m"

local results = Memory.scan("\xFF\xFF")
for i, r in ipairs(results) do
    print(GREEN .. "Library: " .. RESET .. r.library)
    print(YELLOW .. "Offset:  " .. RESET .. string.format("0x%x", r.offset))
    print(CYAN .. "Hex:     " .. RESET .. r.hex)
    print(BLUE .. "ASCII:   " .. RESET .. r.ascii)
    print("---")
end
